- [ ] **Bước 4: Cài đặt tối thiểu `outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/seed/seed_plates.py`**

```python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""Bộ sinh biển số/xe/chủ tất định — bản Python song song plateFor32 (violation-perception-32.js).

Chạy: python seed_plates.py --out plates.json
Thuật toán phải cho KẾT QUẢ GIỐNG HỆT bản JS (test chéo seed-plates-32.test.mjs).
"""
import argparse
import json
import os

SEED_PLATES = 31031
SERIES = 'ABCDEFGHKLMNPRSTUVWXYZ'
COLORS = ['trắng', 'đen', 'xám', 'bạc', 'đỏ', 'xanh dương', 'xanh lá', 'vàng', 'nâu', 'cam', 'hồng', 'tím']
SCENARIO_PLATES = [
    {'slot': 1, 'gen': 0, 'plate': '51K-888.88', 'vehicle_class': 'car'},
    {'slot': 2, 'gen': 0, 'plate': '59-X9 999.99', 'vehicle_class': 'motorbike'},
    {'slot': 3, 'gen': 0, 'plate': '51K-111.11', 'vehicle_class': 'car'},
    {'slot': 4, 'gen': 0, 'plate': '59-A1 222.22', 'vehicle_class': 'motorbike'},
    {'slot': 5, 'gen': 0, 'plate': '51G-333.33', 'vehicle_class': 'car'},
    {'slot': 6, 'gen': 0, 'plate': '59-B2 444.44', 'vehicle_class': 'motorbike'},
]


def hash_u32(n):
    x = (n ^ 0x9E3779B9) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    x = ((x ^ (x >> 16)) * 0x85EBCA6B) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    x = ((x ^ (x >> 13)) * 0xC2B2AE35) & 0xFFFFFFFF
    return (x ^ (x >> 16)) & 0xFFFFFFFF


def mulberry32(a0):
    """Trả về hàm rút word uint32 — khớp mulberry32 bản JS (r() = word / 2**32)."""
    a = a0 & 0xFFFFFFFF

    def nxt():
        nonlocal a
        a = (a + 0x6D2B79F5) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        t1 = ((a ^ (a >> 15)) * (1 | a)) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        inner = ((t1 ^ (t1 >> 7)) * (61 | t1)) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        t2 = (((t1 + inner) & 0xFFFFFFFF) ^ t1) & 0xFFFFFFFF
        return (t2 ^ (t2 >> 14)) & 0xFFFFFFFF

    return nxt


def plate_for(slot, gen, vehicle_class, owners, salt=0):
    r0 = hash_u32((SEED_PLATES ^ slot ^ ((gen << 16) & 0xFFFFFFFF) ^ ((salt * 0x9E3779B9) & 0xFFFFFFFF)) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
    rnd = mulberry32(r0)
    b = rnd()
    letter = SERIES[b % 21]
    b //= 21
    fmt = 'motorbike' if vehicle_class == 'motorbike' else 'car'
    if fmt == 'motorbike':
        d = b % 10
        b //= 10
        n = b % 1000
        b //= 1000
        p = b % 100
        plate = '59-%s%d %03d.%02d' % (letter, d, n, p)
    else:
        with_digit = b % 2
        b //= 2
        mid = ''
        if with_digit:
            mid = str(b % 10)
            b //= 10
        n = b % 1000
        b //= 1000
        p = b % 100
        plate = '51%s%s-%03d.%02d' % (letter, mid, n, p)
    k2 = rnd()
    color = COLORS[(k2 * 12) >> 32]
    k3 = rnd()
    owner_name = owners[(k3 * len(owners)) >> 32] if owners else 'Chủ xe %d' % slot
    k4 = rnd()
    owner_phone = '09%08d' % (k4 % 100000000)
    sc = next((s for s in SCENARIO_PLATES
               if s['slot'] == slot and s['gen'] == gen
               and (('motorbike' if s['vehicle_class'] == 'motorbike' else 'car') == fmt)), None)
    row = {'slot': slot, 'gen': gen, 'salt': salt, 'plate': sc['plate'] if sc else plate,
           'plate_format': fmt, 'vehicle_class': vehicle_class, 'color': color,
           'owner_name': owner_name, 'owner_phone': owner_phone,
           'is_scenario': bool(sc)}
    return row


def generate_all(slot_count=3600, gens=(0, 1, 2), owners=()):
    # Mỗi (slot, gen) có 2 bản ghi theo ĐỊNH DẠNG biển: 'motorbike' và 'car'.
    # bus/truck dùng định dạng 'car' (plateFor32 bản JS cũng vậy) nên lookup theo plate luôn thấy.
    # Slot chạy 1…N (khớp `slot = i + 1` của sim); gen 0…G. `plate` phải DUY NHẤT: khi trùng thì
    # tăng `salt` và sinh lại (cùng công thức trong plateFor32 bản JS) — tất định, chạy lại giống hệt.
    rows = []
    used = set()
    for slot in range(1, slot_count + 1):
        for gen in gens:
            for cls in ('motorbike', 'car'):
                salt = 0
                while True:
                    row = plate_for(slot, gen, cls, owners, salt)
                    if row['plate'] not in used:
                        break
                    salt += 1
                used.add(row['plate'])
                rows.append(row)
    return rows


def main():
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--out', default=os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'plates.json'))
    ap.add_argument('--slots', type=int, default=3600)
    args = ap.parse_args()
    owners_path = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), 'owners.json')
    with open(owners_path, encoding='utf-8') as f:
        owners = json.load(f)
    rows = generate_all(args.slots, (0, 1, 2), owners)
    with open(args.out, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump(rows, f, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(',', ':'))
    print(json.dumps({'rows': len(rows), 'out': args.out}))


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
```

Ghi chú: `plates.json` sinh **2 dòng cho mỗi `(slot, gen)`** theo định dạng biển (`motorbike`, `car` — bus/truck dùng chung định dạng `car`, đúng như `plateFor32` bản JS). **Slot chạy `1…N`** (khớp `slot = i + 1` của sim, `net`/T3), không phải `0…N-1`; **gen `0…G`** với `G` = số `gen` tối đa đo được: chạy `node -e "…createTrafficSim31(net,{breakerS:Infinity,seed:31031}); for(k<steps) step(); …"` trong 600 s (hoặc dài nhất của M1) rồi `Math.max(...sim.gen)`; với cấu hình M1 (600 s, seed 31031) `G = 2` nên tổng **21 600 dòng**; nếu đo lớn hơn thì truyền `gens` lớn hơn và cập nhật `plates_seed` (nạp thêm). Biển **DUY NHẤT**: `generate_all` khử trùng bằng `salt` (sinh lại, tất định). Bảng `plates_seed` khi nạp (Task 5) dùng **PK `(slot, gen, vehicle_class)`** — điểm tự quyết, lệch nhẹ so với mục 4.7 đặc tả (PK `(slot, gen)`), vì một slot có thể mang xe máy hoặc ô tô tùy thứ tự sinh xe của sim; `plate` vẫn UNIQUE. `run_seed.py` (Task 5) chỉ nạp các cặp thực dùng.

```python
import os
import sys
import tempfile
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))


@pytest.fixture()
def client():
    tmp = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix='smart-traffic-test-'))
    os.environ['SMART_DB_PATH'] = str(tmp / 'test.db')
    os.environ['SMART_VAR_DIR'] = str(tmp)
    from app.db import init_db, get_conn
    from app.seed.run_seed import run_seed
    init_db(get_conn())
    run_seed()
    from app import auth
    auth.LOGIN_HITS.clear()      # bộ đếm rate-limit là biến module — reset giữa các test
    auth.LOGIN_LOCKS.clear()
    from app.main import app
    from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
    return TestClient(app)
```

`outputs/smart-traffic/backend/tests/test_auth.py`:

```python
def test_login_seed_account_returns_jwt_with_role(client):
    r = client.post('/api/v1/auth/login', json={'username': 'officer1', 'password': 'SaiGon@2026'})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    body = r.json()
    assert body['token_type'] == 'bearer'
    assert body['role'] == 'officer'
    assert body['expires_in'] == 28800
    me = client.get('/api/v1/auth/me', headers={'Authorization': f"Bearer {body['access_token']}"})
    assert me.status_code == 200
    assert me.json()['role'] == 'officer'


def test_login_wrong_password_401_and_rate_limit_5_per_minute(client):
    for _ in range(5):
        r = client.post('/api/v1/auth/login', json={'username': 'officer1', 'password': 'sai'})
        assert r.status_code == 401
    r = client.post('/api/v1/auth/login', json={'username': 'officer1', 'password': 'SaiGon@2026'})
    assert r.status_code == 429, r.text


def test_role_always_from_jwt_not_from_body(client):
    login = client.post('/api/v1/auth/login', json={'username': 'citizen1', 'password': 'SaiGon@2026'}).json()
    headers = {'Authorization': f"Bearer {login['access_token']}"}
    r = client.post('/api/v1/officer/violations/VP-x/confirm', headers=headers, json={'note': 'x', 'role': 'admin'})
    assert r.status_code in (401, 403)


def test_station_role_login_and_me(client):
    login = client.post('/api/v1/auth/login', json={'username': 'station-3d', 'password': 'SaiGon@2026'}).json()
    assert login['role'] == 'station'
```

```python
import os
from pathlib import Path

BASE_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]          # outputs/smart-traffic/backend
ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]          # outputs/smart-traffic
VAR_DIR = Path(os.environ.get('SMART_VAR_DIR', ROOT_DIR / 'var'))
VAR_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
(VAR_DIR / 'frames').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
(VAR_DIR / 'logs').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
DB_PATH = Path(os.environ.get('SMART_DB_PATH', VAR_DIR / 'smart-traffic.db'))
JWT_SECRET_PATH = VAR_DIR / 'jwt-secret.txt'
JWT_TTL_S = 8 * 3600
LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT = 5          # lần/phút/IP
LOGIN_LOCK_S = 300            # khóa tạm 5 phút
UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024
STATION_RATE_LIMIT = 200      # request/phút/station
BACKEND_PORT = int(os.environ.get('SMART_BACKEND_PORT', 8900))   # đọc env — agent chạy song song tự đổi cổng
STATIC_PORT = int(os.environ.get('PORT', 8821))
VITE_PORT = int(os.environ.get('VITE_PORT', 5173))
ALLOWED_ORIGINS = [o.strip() for o in os.environ.get(
    'SMART_ALLOWED_ORIGINS',
    f'http://127.0.0.1:{STATIC_PORT},http://localhost:{STATIC_PORT},http://127.0.0.1:{VITE_PORT},http://localhost:{VITE_PORT}'
).split(',') if o.strip()]
```

`outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/models.py` — DDL đủ bảng mục 4.7 (bảng M6 tạo sẵn rỗng):

```python
TABLES = [
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, username TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,
        password_hash TEXT NOT NULL,
        role TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (role IN ('citizen','officer','commander','admin','station')),
        display_name TEXT, created_at TEXT, active INTEGER DEFAULT 1)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS citizen_profiles (
        user_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES users(id), full_name TEXT, phone TEXT, email TEXT, created_at TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS plates_seed (
        slot INTEGER, gen INTEGER, vehicle_class TEXT, plate TEXT UNIQUE,
        color TEXT, owner_name TEXT, owner_phone TEXT, is_scenario INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        PRIMARY KEY (slot, gen, vehicle_class))""",   # PK (slot, gen, vehicle_class) — điểm tự quyết, xem Task 4
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vehicles (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, plate TEXT REFERENCES plates_seed(plate),
        vehicle_class TEXT, color TEXT, owner_name TEXT,
        priority_kind TEXT DEFAULT 'none', slot INTEGER, gen INTEGER)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vehicle_ownerships (
        user_id INTEGER REFERENCES users(id), plate TEXT REFERENCES plates_seed(plate),
        created_at TEXT, PRIMARY KEY (user_id, plate))""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS violations (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, violation_code TEXT, status TEXT, plate TEXT, plate_format TEXT,
        vehicle_class TEXT, vehicle_color TEXT, vehicle_id TEXT, cycle_id INTEGER,
        owner_name TEXT, owner_phone TEXT, is_scenario_plate INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
        occurred_at TEXT, node_id TEXT, node_osm_id INTEGER, node_label TEXT, camera_id TEXT,
        road_index INTEGER, lane_index INTEGER, signal_json TEXT, confidence REAL,
        ocr_confidence REAL, detection_json TEXT, penalty_json TEXT, sim_json TEXT,
        priority_flag INTEGER DEFAULT 0, priority_reason TEXT, review_json TEXT,
        reported_to_citizen INTEGER DEFAULT 0, created_at TEXT, updated_at TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS evidence (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, violation_id TEXT REFERENCES violations(id), kind TEXT,
        media_id TEXT REFERENCES media_assets(id), captured_at TEXT, width INTEGER, height INTEGER, sha256 TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS media_assets (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, kind TEXT, path TEXT, mime TEXT, bytes INTEGER, sha256 TEXT,
        camera_id TEXT, created_at TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS complaints (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, violation_id TEXT REFERENCES violations(id), user_id INTEGER REFERENCES users(id),
        message TEXT, status TEXT, resolution TEXT, created_at TEXT, resolved_at TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS notifications (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, user_id INTEGER REFERENCES users(id), violation_id TEXT REFERENCES violations(id),
        kind TEXT, title TEXT, body TEXT, read_at TEXT, created_at TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS payments (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, violation_id TEXT REFERENCES violations(id), amount_vnd INTEGER,
        status TEXT, created_at TEXT, by_user INTEGER REFERENCES users(id))""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS signal_nodes (
        node_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, osm_id INTEGER, label TEXT, cluster_id TEXT, x REAL, z REAL,
        camera_id TEXT REFERENCES cameras(camera_id), has_vms INTEGER DEFAULT 0, prefix TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cameras (
        camera_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, node_id TEXT REFERENCES signal_nodes(node_id), x REAL, z REAL,
        height_m REAL, yaw_deg REAL, pitch_deg REAL, fov_deg REAL, range_m REAL, label TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS signal_state_log (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, node_id TEXT, cluster_id TEXT, t_sim REAL,
        phase TEXT, grp INTEGER, mode TEXT, override_by TEXT, reason TEXT, created_at TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS kpi_samples (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, t_sim REAL, window_s REAL, wait_avg_s REAL,
        wait_p95_s REAL, queue_max REAL, queue_avg REAL, flow_per_min REAL, mode TEXT, scenario_key TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS incidents (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, segment_id TEXT, level TEXT, prob REAL, reliability REAL,
        message TEXT, t_sim REAL, created_at TEXT, cleared_at TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS vms_messages (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, vms_id TEXT, lines_json TEXT, ttl_s INTEGER, active INTEGER,
        created_by INTEGER REFERENCES users(id), created_at TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS station_metrics (
        id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, station_id TEXT, t TEXT, payload_json TEXT, created_at TEXT)""",
    """CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS scenario_runs (
        id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, key TEXT, seed INTEGER, mode TEXT, params_json TEXT,
        result_json TEXT, started_at TEXT, finished_at TEXT)""",
    "CREATE UNIQUE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ux_violations_dup ON violations(vehicle_id, node_id, cycle_id, violation_code)",
    "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_violations_status ON violations(status, occurred_at)",
    "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_violations_plate ON violations(plate, occurred_at)",
    "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_violations_camera ON violations(camera_id, occurred_at)",
    "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_cameras_node ON cameras(node_id)",
    "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_kpi_t ON kpi_samples(t_sim)",
    "CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS ix_notif_user ON notifications(user_id, read_at)",
]
```

`outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/db.py`:

```python
import sqlite3
from app import config
from app.models import TABLES


def get_conn():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(config.DB_PATH)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    conn.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
    conn.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON')
    return conn


def init_db(conn):
    for ddl in TABLES:
        conn.execute(ddl)
    conn.commit()
```

`outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/auth.py`:

```python
import base64
import hashlib
import hmac
import json
import os
import time

import bcrypt
import jwt
from fastapi import HTTPException, Request

from app import config

LOGIN_HITS = {}      # ip -> [epoch, count]
LOGIN_LOCKS = {}     # ip -> locked_until_epoch


def hash_password(pw: str) -> str:
    return bcrypt.hashpw(pw.encode('utf-8'), bcrypt.gensalt(rounds=12)).decode('utf-8')


def verify_password(pw: str, hashed: str) -> bool:
    try:
        return bcrypt.checkpw(pw.encode('utf-8'), hashed.encode('utf-8'))
    except ValueError:
        return False


def load_or_create_secret() -> str:
    p = config.JWT_SECRET_PATH
    if p.exists():
        return p.read_text(encoding='utf-8').strip()
    secret = base64.b64encode(os.urandom(48)).decode('ascii')
    p.write_text(secret, encoding='utf-8')
    try:
        os.chmod(p, 0o600)
    except OSError:
        pass
    return secret


def make_token(user) -> str:
    now = int(time.time())
    payload = {'sub': user['username'], 'role': user['role'], 'iat': now, 'exp': now + config.JWT_TTL_S}
    return jwt.encode(payload, load_or_create_secret(), algorithm='HS256')


def decode_token(token: str) -> dict:
    try:
        return jwt.decode(token, load_or_create_secret(), algorithms=['HS256'])
    except jwt.PyJWTError:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail={'error': {'code': 'UNAUTHORIZED', 'message': 'Token không hợp lệ', 'detail': None}})


def bearer_of(request: Request) -> str:
    auth = request.headers.get('Authorization', '')
    if not auth.startswith('Bearer '):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail={'error': {'code': 'UNAUTHORIZED', 'message': 'Thiếu token', 'detail': None}})
    return auth[7:]


def require_role(*roles):
    def dep(request: Request):
        claims = decode_token(bearer_of(request))
        if claims.get('role') not in roles:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail={'error': {'code': 'FORBIDDEN', 'message': 'Không đủ quyền', 'detail': None}})
        request.state.claims = claims
        return claims
    return dep


class LoginLimiter:
    @staticmethod
    def check(ip: str):
        now = time.time()
        until = LOGIN_LOCKS.get(ip, 0)
        if now < until:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail={'error': {'code': 'RATE_LIMITED', 'message': 'Đăng nhập quá nhiều, thử lại sau 5 phút', 'detail': None}})
        window, count = LOGIN_HITS.get(ip, (now, 0))
        if now - window >= 60:
            window, count = now, 0
        count += 1
        LOGIN_HITS[ip] = (window, count)
        if count > config.LOGIN_RATE_LIMIT:
            LOGIN_LOCKS[ip] = now + config.LOGIN_LOCK_S
            raise HTTPException(status_code=429, detail={'error': {'code': 'RATE_LIMITED', 'message': 'Đăng nhập quá nhiều, thử lại sau 5 phút', 'detail': None}})
```

`outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/seed/run_seed.py`:

```python
# Nạp seed dev vào SQLite: tài khoản, biển số (seed_plates), node_map, cameras.
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

SEED_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(SEED_DIR.parent.parent))   # outputs/smart-traffic/backend


def run_seed():
    from app.db import get_conn
    from app.auth import hash_password
    from app.seed.seed_plates import generate_all
    conn = get_conn()
    # Thứ tự ghi TÔN TRỌNG khoá ngoại (PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON): bảng được tham chiếu trước.
    accounts = json.loads((SEED_DIR / 'accounts.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    for a in accounts:
        conn.execute(
            'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO users (username, password_hash, role, display_name, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,datetime("now"))',
            (a['username'], hash_password(a['password']), a['role'], a['display_name']))
    # 1) plates_seed (được vehicle_ownerships tham chiếu).
    owners = json.loads((SEED_DIR / 'owners.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    rows = generate_all(3600, (0, 1, 2), owners)
    conn.executemany(
        'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO plates_seed (slot, gen, vehicle_class, plate, color, owner_name, owner_phone, is_scenario) VALUES (:slot,:gen,:vehicle_class,:plate,:color,:owner_name,:owner_phone,:is_scenario)',
        [{**r, 'is_scenario': int(r['is_scenario'])} for r in rows])
    # 2) cameras (được signal_nodes.camera_id tham chiếu).
    node_map = json.loads((SEED_DIR / 'node_map.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    cameras = json.loads((SEED_DIR / 'cameras.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    conn.executemany(
        'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO cameras (camera_id, node_id, x, z, height_m, yaw_deg, pitch_deg, fov_deg, range_m, label) VALUES (:camera_id,:node_id,:x,:z,:height_m,:yaw_deg,:pitch_deg,:fov_deg,:range_m,:label)',
        cameras)
    # 3) signal_nodes: ghi camera_id = NULL trước (cameras có thể chưa có ở môi trường khác), rồi UPDATE.
    conn.executemany(
        'INSERT OR IGNORE INTO signal_nodes (node_id, osm_id, label, cluster_id, x, z, camera_id, has_vms, prefix) VALUES (:node_id,:osm_id,:label,:cluster_id,:x,:z,NULL,:has_vms,:prefix)',
        [{**n, 'has_vms': int(n['has_vms'])} for n in node_map])
    for n in node_map:
        if n.get('camera_id'):
            conn.execute('UPDATE signal_nodes SET camera_id=? WHERE node_id=?', (n['camera_id'], n['node_id']))
    # 4) vehicle_ownerships SAU khi có plates_seed.
    for a in accounts:
        if a.get('plates'):
            row = conn.execute('SELECT id FROM users WHERE username=?', (a['username'],)).fetchone()
            for plate in a['plates']:
                conn.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO vehicle_ownerships (user_id, plate, created_at) VALUES (?,?,datetime("now"))', (row['id'], plate))
    conn.commit()
    conn.close()


if __name__ == '__main__':
    run_seed()
    print(json.dumps({'ok': True}))
```

`outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/main.py` (bản Task 5 — Task 6/7 sẽ mount thêm router):

```python
from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware

from app import config
from app.auth import LoginLimiter, make_token, require_role, verify_password
from app.db import get_conn
from app.schemas import LoginIn

app = FastAPI(title='SaiGon SMTF · Trung tâm điều hành giao thông khu Bắc', version='32a')
app.add_middleware(CORSMiddleware, allow_origins=config.ALLOWED_ORIGINS, allow_credentials=True,
                   allow_methods=['*'], allow_headers=['*'])


@app.post('/api/v1/auth/login')
def login(body: LoginIn, request: Request):
    LoginLimiter.check(request.client.host if request.client else '0.0.0.0')
    conn = get_conn()
    row = conn.execute('SELECT * FROM users WHERE username=? AND active=1', (body.username,)).fetchone()
    if not row or not verify_password(body.password, row['password_hash']):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=401, detail={'error': {'code': 'UNAUTHORIZED', 'message': 'Sai tài khoản hoặc mật khẩu', 'detail': None}})
    return {'access_token': make_token(row), 'token_type': 'bearer', 'expires_in': config.JWT_TTL_S,
            'role': row['role'], 'display_name': row['display_name']}


@app.post('/api/v1/auth/logout')
def logout():
    return {'ok': True}


@app.get('/api/v1/auth/me')
def me(claims=Depends(require_role('citizen', 'officer', 'commander', 'admin', 'station'))):
    conn = get_conn()
    row = conn.execute('SELECT id, username, role, display_name FROM users WHERE username=?', (claims['sub'],)).fetchone()
    plates = [r['plate'] for r in conn.execute('SELECT plate FROM vehicle_ownerships WHERE user_id=?', (row['id'],))]
    return {'id': row['id'], 'username': row['username'], 'role': row['role'],
            'display_name': row['display_name'], 'plates': plates}
```

(Lỗi HTTP thống nhất dạng `{"error": {"code", "message", "detail"}}` — các router Task 6/7 dùng `app.errors.err`.)

```python
def test_seed_accounts_and_plates(client):
    from app.db import get_conn
    conn = get_conn()
    users = {r['username']: r['role'] for r in conn.execute('SELECT username, role FROM users')}
    for u, role in [('admin1', 'admin'), ('commander1', 'commander'), ('officer1', 'officer'), ('citizen1', 'citizen'), ('station-3d', 'station')]:
        assert users.get(u) == role
    n = conn.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM plates_seed').fetchone()['n']
    assert n == 21600
    sc = conn.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM plates_seed WHERE is_scenario=1').fetchone()['n']
    assert sc == 6
    cams = {r['camera_id'] for r in conn.execute('SELECT camera_id FROM cameras')}
    assert cams == {'CAM-HX-001', 'CAM-HX-002'}
    nodes = {r['node_id'] for r in conn.execute('SELECT node_id FROM signal_nodes')}
    assert nodes == {'HX-01', 'HX-02', 'HX-03', 'HX-04', 'HX-05', 'HX-06'}


def test_plates_format_valid(client):
    import re
    from app.db import get_conn
    conn = get_conn()
    rows = conn.execute('SELECT plate, vehicle_class FROM plates_seed LIMIT 50').fetchall()
    for r in rows:
        if r['vehicle_class'] == 'motorbike':
            assert re.match(r'^[0-9]{2}[A-Z][0-9] [0-9]{3}\.[0-9]{2}$', r['plate'])
        else:
            assert re.match(r'^[0-9]{2}[A-Z][0-9]?-[0-9]{3}\.[0-9]{2}$', r['plate'])
```

---

### Task 6: API trạm + vi phạm + bằng chứng + WS + API công an

**Files:**
- Create: `outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/ws.py`, `app/routers/__init__.py`, `app/routers/station.py`, `app/routers/officer.py`
- Modify: `outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/main.py` (khoảng dòng 1–10 và cuối file — mount router + WS)
- Test: `outputs/smart-traffic/backend/tests/test_station.py`, `tests/test_officer.py`, `tests/test_ws.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `app.db.get_conn`, `app.auth.require_role`, `app.errors.err`, schema bản ghi vi phạm mục 4.4 (Task 3 đã định nghĩa shape).
- Produces (REST, base `http://localhost:8900/api/v1`):
  - `POST /station/heartbeat` body `{station_id, app_version, t, uptime_s, fps, webgpu}` → `{ok, server_time, commands}` (role `station`).
  - `POST /station/metrics` body `{station_id, t, fps, draw_calls, traffic:{active, waiting_now, edge_transitions}, kpi:{wait_avg_s, queue_max, flow_per_min}, signal_mode}` (subset mục 6.3) → `{ok}`; ghi `kpi_samples` + `station_metrics`.
  - `POST /station/violations` body = bản ghi mục 4.4 thiếu `id/created_at/updated_at` → `{id, status}`; sinh `id = VP-YYYYMMDD-NNNNNN`, `occurred_at = server_time` (ISO UTC); 409 nếu trùng `(vehicle_id, node_id, cycle_id, violation_code)`; tự đặt `status = confidence >= 0.85 ? 'new' : 'needs_review'`, `priority_flag` → `needs_review`.
  - `POST /station/violations/{id}/evidence` `multipart/form-data` (`file` + trường `kind ∈ {before, after, plate_crop}`) → `{media_id, sha256}`; ghi `media_assets` + `evidence`.
  - `POST /station/media` `multipart/form-data` — tên file phải khớp `^(CAM-[A-Z]{2,3}-[0-9]{3})\.(mp4|jpg|png)$` → `{media_id, camera_id}`; 422 nếu sai; chặn path traversal.
  - `POST /station/frames` body `{camera_id, seq, t, jpeg_base64, w, h}` → `{ok}`; giữ frame mới nhất/camera trong RAM + `var/frames/`.
  - `GET /officer/violations?status=&code=&camera_id=&from=&to=&page=&page_size=` → `{items, total, page}` (role `officer|commander|admin`).
  - `GET /officer/violations/{id}` → bản ghi đầy đủ + `evidence`.
  - `POST /officer/violations/{id}/confirm|dismiss|needs-review` body `{note}` → bản ghi đổi `status` (M1 chỉ 4 trạng thái; chuyển sai → 409); ghi `review_json`; phát `violation.updated`.
  - `GET /officer/kpi?from=&to=&node_id=` → `{wait_avg_s, queue_max, flow_per_min, mode, samples}`.
  - `GET /officer/streams` → `{streams: [{camera_id, fps, url, active}]}`.
  - `POST /officer/controls/signal-mode` body `{mode}` (role `commander|admin`) → `{ok, mode}`; phát `cmd.signal-mode` tới trạm.
  - `POST /officer/controls/signal-override` body `{node_id, group, duration_s, reason}` (`group: null` = hủy) → `{ok, until_t_sim, until_at}`; phát `cmd.signal-override`.
  - `GET /officer/controls/state` → `{mode, overrides, scenario}`.
- Produces (WS `ws://localhost:8900/ws/v1`): auth bằng tin nhắn đầu `{"type":"auth","token":"<JWT>"}` trong 5 s (quá → đóng kết nối); thông điệp `{event, t, seq, data}`. **Allowlist theo vai trò**: CHỈ role `station` được phát `station.heartbeat`, `station.metrics`, `station.frame`, `violation.detected`, `violation.evidence` (broadcast tới `officer|commander|admin`); mọi event khác — kể cả `cmd.*` — bị bỏ qua + log. `cmd.signal-mode`, `cmd.signal-override`, `cmd.reload-seed` CHỈ sinh từ REST `officer/controls/*` (role `commander|admin`) và broadcast tới `station`. Backend phát cho web `violation.created`, `violation.updated`, `kpi.update`, `signal.state`, `stream.frame`.
- REST handler có broadcast đều `async def` và gọi `await broadcast(...)` (không dùng thread `broadcast_sync`); `POST /officer/controls/signal-override` nhận `until_t_sim` (giây sim) từ body, lưu `OVERRIDES` và trả trong `GET /officer/controls/state` (station báo trạng thái thật qua `station.metrics.overrides`).

**Phụ thuộc:** Task 5. Chạy song song được với Task 8, Task 12.

**Model đề xuất:** `deepseek-v4.1-flash`

```python
        'vehicle_color': 'trắng', 'vehicle_id': 'V-004231', 'owner_name': 'Nguyễn Văn A', 'owner_phone': '0901234567',
        'is_scenario_plate': False, 'node_id': 'HX-01', 'node_osm_id': 366390689, 'node_label': 'Cầu vượt Hàng Xanh ∩ Điện Biên Phủ',
        'camera_id': 'CAM-HX-001', 'road_index': 1287, 'lane_index': 4, 'cycle_id': 12,
        'signal': {'mode': 'smart', 'cluster_id': 'HX-C1', 'cycle_id': 12, 'phase_for_vehicle': 'red', 'group': 1,
                   'elapsed_in_phase_s': 2.4, 'cycle_s': 48.0, 'speed_kmh': 38.5, 'speed_limit_kmh': 50},
        'confidence': 0.9, 'ocr_confidence': 0.91,
        'detection': {'in_fov_s': 3.2, 'distance_m': 41.0, 'grace_s': 0.8, 'crossed_stop_line_at_s': 102.4},
        'evidence': [],
        'penalty': {'vnd_min': 18000000, 'vnd_max': 20000000, 'license_deduction_points': 4,
                    'source': 'NĐ 168/2024 · Điều 6 · khoản 9 · điểm b', 'verified': True},
        'sim': {'t_sim': 102.4, 'seed': 31031, 'scenario': 'peak-hang-xanh', 'station_id': 'STATION-3D-01', 'station_time': '2026-10-14T09:29:58.2Z'},
    }
    body.update(over)
    return body


def test_create_violation_and_dedup(client):
    h = station_headers(client)
    r = client.post('/api/v1/station/violations', headers=h, json=violation_body())
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    vid = r.json()['id']
    assert vid.startswith('VP-')
    assert r.json()['status'] == 'new'
    again = client.post('/api/v1/station/violations', headers=h, json=violation_body())
    assert again.status_code == 409


def test_low_confidence_goes_needs_review(client):
    h = station_headers(client)
    body = violation_body(confidence=0.5, vehicle_id='V-000002', cycle_id=3)
    r = client.post('/api/v1/station/violations', headers=h, json=body)
    assert r.json()['status'] == 'needs_review'


def test_evidence_upload_and_media_name_validation(client):
    h = station_headers(client)
    vid = client.post('/api/v1/station/violations', headers=h, json=violation_body(vehicle_id='V-000003', cycle_id=4)).json()['id']
    png = base64.b64decode('iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAAEAAAABCAYAAAAfFcSJAAAADUlEQVR42mP8z8BQDwAEhQGAhKmMIQAAAABJRU5ErkJggg==')
    r = client.post(f'/api/v1/station/violations/{vid}/evidence', headers=h,
                    files={'file': ('frame.png', io.BytesIO(png), 'image/png')}, data={'kind': 'before'})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert r.json()['media_id'].startswith('MD-')
    bad = client.post('/api/v1/station/media', headers=h,
                      files={'file': ('../../evil.jpg', io.BytesIO(png), 'image/jpeg')})
    assert bad.status_code == 422
    good = client.post('/api/v1/station/media', headers=h,
                       files={'file': ('CAM-HX-001.jpg', io.BytesIO(png), 'image/jpeg')})
    assert good.status_code == 200
    assert good.json()['camera_id'] == 'CAM-HX-001'


def test_heartbeat_and_metrics(client):
    h = station_headers(client)
    r = client.post('/api/v1/station/heartbeat', headers=h, json={
        'station_id': 'STATION-3D-01', 'app_version': '32a', 't': '2026-10-14T09:30:00Z',
        'uptime_s': 1200, 'fps': 58.2, 'webgpu': True})
    assert r.status_code == 200
    assert 'server_time' in r.json() and 'commands' in r.json()
    m = client.post('/api/v1/station/metrics', headers=h, json={
        'station_id': 'STATION-3D-01', 't': '2026-10-14T09:30:05Z', 'fps': 58.0, 'draw_calls': 220,
        'traffic': {'active': 800, 'waiting_now': 40, 'edge_transitions': 1200},
        'kpi': {'wait_avg_s': 12.5, 'queue_max': 18, 'flow_per_min': 96.0}, 'signal_mode': 'smart'})
    assert m.status_code == 200
```

`outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/ws.py`:

```python
# Hub WebSocket theo vai trò — auth bằng tin nhắn đầu {"type":"auth","token"} trong 5 s.
import asyncio
import json
import time

from app.auth import decode_token

SEQ = 0
CLIENTS = []   # {ws, role, username}
# Allowlist chiều trạm → backend: CHỈ `station` gửi các event này. `cmd.*` KHÔNG nhận từ WS
# (chỉ sinh ở REST officer/commander/admin) nên không có đường leo quyền điều khiển đèn.
STATION_EVENTS = {'station.heartbeat', 'station.metrics', 'station.frame', 'violation.detected', 'violation.evidence'}


def _next_seq():
    global SEQ
    SEQ += 1
    return SEQ


async def ws_handler(websocket):
    role = None
    try:
        raw = await asyncio.wait_for(websocket.receive_text(), timeout=5)
        msg = json.loads(raw)
        if msg.get('type') != 'auth':
            await websocket.close(code=4401)
            return
        claims = decode_token(msg.get('token', ''))
        role = claims['role']
        CLIENTS.append({'ws': websocket, 'role': role, 'username': claims['sub']})
        while True:
            data = await websocket.receive_text()
            payload = json.loads(data)
            event = payload.get('event')
            if not event:
                continue
            if role != 'station' or event not in STATION_EVENTS:
                print(f'[ws] bỏ qua event không hợp lệ: role={role} event={event}', flush=True)
                continue
            await broadcast(event, payload.get('data', {}), to_roles=['officer', 'commander', 'admin'])
    except (asyncio.TimeoutError, Exception):
        pass
    finally:
        CLIENTS[:] = [c for c in CLIENTS if c['ws'] is not websocket]


async def broadcast(event: str, data: dict, to_roles=None):
    import datetime
    payload = json.dumps({'event': event, 't': datetime.datetime.utcnow().isoformat() + 'Z',
                          'seq': _next_seq(), 'data': data}, ensure_ascii=False)
    dead = []
    for c in CLIENTS:
        if to_roles is not None and c['role'] not in to_roles:
            continue
        try:
            await c['ws'].send_text(payload)
        except Exception:
            dead.append(c)
    for c in dead:
        CLIENTS[:] = [x for x in CLIENTS if x is not c]


def broadcast_sync(event: str, data: dict, to_roles=None):
    """CHỈ dùng khi thật sự ở thread/loop khác (ví dụ watchdog). Endpoint của app là `async def`
    và gọi thẳng `await broadcast(...)` — KHÔNG dùng hàm này trong request handler."""
    try:
        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
    except RuntimeError:
        return None
    return asyncio.run_coroutine_threadsafe(broadcast(event, data, to_roles), loop)
```

```python
import base64
import hashlib
import json
import re
import secrets
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, File, Form, HTTPException, Request, UploadFile

from app import config
from app.auth import require_role
from app.db import get_conn
from app.errors import err
from app.ws import broadcast

router = APIRouter(prefix='/api/v1/station', tags=['station'])
STATION = require_role('station')
MEDIA_NAME = re.compile(r'^(CAM-[A-Z]{2,3}-[0-9]{3})\.(mp4|jpg|png)$')
FRAMES = {}   # camera_id → frame mới nhất


def _now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace('+00:00', 'Z')


@router.post('/heartbeat')
async def heartbeat(body: dict, claims=Depends(STATION)):
    return {'ok': True, 'server_time': _now(), 'commands': []}


@router.post('/metrics')
async def metrics(body: dict, claims=Depends(STATION)):
    conn = get_conn()
    kpi = body.get('kpi', {})
    conn.execute('INSERT INTO kpi_samples (t_sim, window_s, wait_avg_s, wait_p95_s, queue_max, queue_avg, flow_per_min, mode, scenario_key) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                 (body.get('t_sim', body.get('t')), 60, kpi.get('wait_avg_s', 0), 0, kpi.get('queue_max', 0), 0, kpi.get('flow_per_min', 0), body.get('signal_mode', 'fixed'), None))
    conn.execute('INSERT INTO station_metrics (station_id, t, payload_json, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?)',
                 (body.get('station_id'), body.get('t'), json.dumps(body, ensure_ascii=False), _now()))
    conn.commit()
    # Trạm báo lại danh sách override đang áp dụng (kèm `until_t_sim` theo đồng hồ sim) → lưu trạng thái.
    ovs = body.get('overrides')
    if isinstance(ovs, list):
        from app.routers.officer import OVERRIDES
        live = {o.get('node_id') for o in ovs if o.get('node_id')}
        for o in ovs:
            if o.get('node_id'):
                OVERRIDES[o['node_id']] = o
        for nid in list(OVERRIDES):
            if nid not in live:
                OVERRIDES.pop(nid, None)
    await broadcast('kpi.update', {'t': body.get('t'), 'wait_avg_s': kpi.get('wait_avg_s', 0),
                                   'queue_max': kpi.get('queue_max', 0), 'flow_per_min': kpi.get('flow_per_min', 0),
                                   'mode': body.get('signal_mode', 'fixed')}, to_roles=['officer', 'commander', 'admin'])
    return {'ok': True}


@router.post('/violations')
async def create_violation(body: dict, claims=Depends(STATION)):
    conn = get_conn()
    # cycle_id nằm ở `signal.cycle_id` (shape bản ghi T3); fallback cấp gốc cho tương thích.
    cycle_id = (body.get('signal') or {}).get('cycle_id', body.get('cycle_id'))
    dup = conn.execute('SELECT id FROM violations WHERE vehicle_id=? AND node_id=? AND cycle_id=? AND violation_code=?',
                       (body.get('vehicle_id'), body.get('node_id'), cycle_id, body.get('violation_code'))).fetchone()
    if dup:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=err('CONFLICT', 'Bản ghi trùng (vehicle_id, node_id, cycle_id, violation_code)'))
    day = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime('%Y%m%d')
    n = conn.execute("SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM violations WHERE id LIKE ?", (f'VP-{day}-%',)).fetchone()['n'] + 1
    vid = f'VP-{day}-{n:06d}'
    now = _now()
    status = 'needs_review' if body.get('priority_flag') or body.get('confidence', 0) < 0.85 else 'new'
    conn.execute('''INSERT INTO violations (id, violation_code, status, plate, plate_format, vehicle_class, vehicle_color,
        vehicle_id, cycle_id, owner_name, owner_phone, is_scenario_plate, occurred_at, node_id, node_osm_id, node_label,
        camera_id, road_index, lane_index, signal_json, confidence, ocr_confidence, detection_json, penalty_json, sim_json,
        priority_flag, priority_reason, review_json, created_at, updated_at)
        VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)''',
        (vid, body.get('violation_code'), status, body.get('plate'), body.get('plate_format'), body.get('vehicle_class'),
         body.get('vehicle_color'), body.get('vehicle_id'), cycle_id, body.get('owner_name'), body.get('owner_phone'),
         int(bool(body.get('is_scenario_plate'))), now, body.get('node_id'), body.get('node_osm_id'), body.get('node_label'),
         body.get('camera_id'), body.get('road_index'), body.get('lane_index'), json.dumps(body.get('signal', {}), ensure_ascii=False),
         body.get('confidence', 0), body.get('ocr_confidence', 0), json.dumps(body.get('detection', {}), ensure_ascii=False),
         json.dumps(body.get('penalty', {}), ensure_ascii=False), json.dumps(body.get('sim', {}), ensure_ascii=False),
         int(bool(body.get('priority_flag'))), body.get('priority_reason'), json.dumps({'by': None, 'at': None, 'note': None}), now, now))
    conn.commit()
    row = conn.execute('SELECT * FROM violations WHERE id=?', (vid,)).fetchone()
    await broadcast('violation.created', {'id': vid, 'status': status, 'plate': row['plate'],
                                          'violation_code': row['violation_code'], 'occurred_at': now,
                                          'node_id': row['node_id'], 'camera_id': row['camera_id'],
                                          'confidence': row['confidence']}, to_roles=['officer', 'commander', 'admin'])
    return {'id': vid, 'status': status}


@router.post('/violations/{vid}/evidence')
async def upload_evidence(vid: str, file: UploadFile = File(...), kind: str = Form(...), claims=Depends(STATION)):
    if kind not in ('before', 'after', 'plate_crop'):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=err('VALIDATION', 'kind phải là before|after|plate_crop'))
    conn = get_conn()
    if not conn.execute('SELECT id FROM violations WHERE id=?', (vid,)).fetchone():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=err('NOT_FOUND', 'Không có vi phạm'))
    data = file.file.read(config.UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES + 1)
    if len(data) > config.UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail=err('TOO_LARGE', 'File quá 50 MB'))
    sha = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    mid = f'MD-{secrets.token_hex(8)}'   # id ngẫu nhiên khó đoán
    path = config.VAR_DIR / 'frames' / f'{mid}.png'
    path.write_bytes(data)
    conn.execute('INSERT INTO media_assets (id, kind, path, mime, bytes, sha256, camera_id, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                 (mid, 'image', str(path), file.content_type or 'image/png', len(data), sha, None, _now()))
    eid = f'EV-{vid}-{kind}'
    conn.execute('INSERT INTO evidence (id, violation_id, kind, media_id, captured_at, width, height, sha256) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                 (eid, vid, kind, mid, _now(), 0, 0, sha))
    conn.commit()
    await broadcast('violation.evidence', {'violation_id': vid, 'kind': kind, 'media_id': mid, 'sha256': sha}, to_roles=['officer', 'commander', 'admin'])
    return {'media_id': mid, 'sha256': sha}


@router.post('/media')
async def upload_media(file: UploadFile = File(...), claims=Depends(STATION)):
    name = Path(file.filename or '').name
    if '/' in name or '\\' in name or '..' in name:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=err('VALIDATION', 'Tên file không hợp lệ'))
    m = MEDIA_NAME.match(name)
    if not m:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=err('VALIDATION', 'Tên file phải khớp ^(CAM-[A-Z]{2,3}-[0-9]{3})\\.(mp4|jpg|png)$'))
    data = file.file.read(config.UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES + 1)
    if len(data) > config.UPLOAD_MAX_BYTES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=413, detail=err('TOO_LARGE', 'File quá 50 MB'))
    sha = hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
    mid = f'MD-{secrets.token_hex(8)}'
    dest = config.VAR_DIR / 'frames' / name
    dest.write_bytes(data)
    conn = get_conn()
    conn.execute('INSERT INTO media_assets (id, kind, path, mime, bytes, sha256, camera_id, created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                 (mid, 'video' if name.endswith('.mp4') else 'image', str(dest), file.content_type or 'application/octet-stream',
                  len(data), sha, m.group(1), _now()))
    conn.commit()
    return {'media_id': mid, 'camera_id': m.group(1)}


@router.post('/frames')
async def post_frame(body: dict, claims=Depends(STATION)):
    cam = body.get('camera_id', '')
    if not MEDIA_NAME.match(f'{cam}.jpg'):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=err('VALIDATION', 'camera_id không hợp lệ'))
    raw = base64.b64decode(body.get('jpeg_base64', ''))
    FRAMES[cam] = {'seq': body.get('seq'), 't': body.get('t'), 'w': body.get('w'), 'h': body.get('h'), 'jpeg': raw}
    (config.VAR_DIR / 'frames' / f'{cam}-latest.jpg').write_bytes(raw)
    await broadcast('stream.frame', {'camera_id': cam, 'seq': body.get('seq'), 'jpeg_base64': body.get('jpeg_base64')},
                    to_roles=['officer', 'commander', 'admin'])
    return {'ok': True}
```

`outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/routers/officer.py`:

```python
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException

from app.auth import require_role
from app.db import get_conn
from app.errors import err
from app.schemas import ReviewIn
from app.ws import broadcast

router = APIRouter(prefix='/api/v1/officer', tags=['officer'])
OFFICER = require_role('officer', 'commander', 'admin')
COMMANDER = require_role('commander', 'admin')
# Cạnh hợp lệ M1 = đúng sơ đồ spec 4.4: confirmed/dismissed KHÔNG có cạnh ra trong M1
# (notified/paid/disputed thuộc M6). Không có `new → paid`, không có `confirmed → dismissed`.
ALLOWED = {'new': {'confirmed', 'dismissed', 'needs_review'},
           'needs_review': {'confirmed', 'dismissed'},
           'confirmed': set(),
           'dismissed': set()}
OVERRIDES = {}   # node_id → {group, by, reason, until_t_sim, until_at} — trạng thái ghi đè đang có


def _now():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace('+00:00', 'Z')


@router.get('/violations')
def list_violations(status: str = '', code: str = '', camera_id: str = '', page: int = 1, page_size: int = 50, claims=Depends(OFFICER)):
    conn = get_conn()
    where, args = [], []
    if status:
        where.append('status=?'); args.append(status)
    if code:
        where.append('violation_code=?'); args.append(code)
    if camera_id:
        where.append('camera_id=?'); args.append(camera_id)
    sql = 'SELECT id, violation_code, status, plate, vehicle_class, occurred_at, node_id, camera_id, confidence, penalty_json FROM violations'
    if where:
        sql += ' WHERE ' + ' AND '.join(where)
    total = conn.execute(f'SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM ({sql})', args).fetchone()['n']
    rows = conn.execute(sql + ' ORDER BY occurred_at DESC LIMIT ? OFFSET ?', (*args, page_size, (page - 1) * page_size)).fetchall()
    items = []
    for r in rows:
        item = dict(r)
        item['penalty'] = json.loads(item.pop('penalty_json') or 'null')   # UI hiển thị mức phạt
        items.append(item)
    return {'items': items, 'total': total, 'page': page}


@router.get('/violations/{vid}')
def get_violation(vid: str, claims=Depends(OFFICER)):
    conn = get_conn()
    row = conn.execute('SELECT * FROM violations WHERE id=?', (vid,)).fetchone()
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=err('NOT_FOUND', 'Không có vi phạm'))
    out = dict(row)
    for key in ('signal_json', 'detection_json', 'penalty_json', 'sim_json', 'review_json'):
        out[key.replace('_json', '')] = json.loads(out.pop(key) or 'null')
    out['evidence'] = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute('SELECT * FROM evidence WHERE violation_id=?', (vid,))]
    return out


async def _review(vid: str, target: str, note: str, claims):
    conn = get_conn()
    row = conn.execute('SELECT * FROM violations WHERE id=?', (vid,)).fetchone()
    if not row:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=err('NOT_FOUND', 'Không có vi phạm'))
    if target not in ALLOWED.get(row['status'], set()):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=409, detail=err('CONFLICT', f"Không thể chuyển {row['status']} → {target}"))
    review = {'by': claims['sub'], 'at': _now(), 'note': note}
    conn.execute('UPDATE violations SET status=?, review_json=?, updated_at=? WHERE id=?',
                 (target, json.dumps(review, ensure_ascii=False), _now(), vid))
    conn.commit()
    await broadcast('violation.updated', {'id': vid, 'status': target, 'review': review}, to_roles=['officer', 'commander', 'admin'])
    return {'id': vid, 'status': target, 'review': review}


@router.post('/violations/{vid}/confirm')
async def confirm(vid: str, body: ReviewIn, claims=Depends(OFFICER)):
    return await _review(vid, 'confirmed', body.note, claims)


@router.post('/violations/{vid}/dismiss')
async def dismiss(vid: str, body: ReviewIn, claims=Depends(OFFICER)):
    return await _review(vid, 'dismissed', body.note, claims)


@router.post('/violations/{vid}/needs-review')
async def needs_review(vid: str, body: ReviewIn, claims=Depends(OFFICER)):
    return await _review(vid, 'needs_review', body.note, claims)


@router.get('/kpi')
def kpi(from_: str = '', to: str = '', node_id: str = '', claims=Depends(OFFICER)):
    conn = get_conn()
    where, args = ['1=1'], []
    if from_:
        where.append('t_sim>=?'); args.append(from_)
    if to:
        where.append('t_sim<=?'); args.append(to)
    rows = conn.execute('SELECT t_sim, wait_avg_s, queue_max, flow_per_min, mode FROM kpi_samples WHERE ' + ' AND '.join(where) + ' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 120', args).fetchall()
    samples = [dict(r) for r in rows]
    last = samples[0] if samples else {'wait_avg_s': 0, 'queue_max': 0, 'flow_per_min': 0, 'mode': 'fixed'}
    return {'wait_avg_s': last['wait_avg_s'], 'queue_max': last['queue_max'],
            'flow_per_min': last['flow_per_min'], 'mode': last['mode'], 'samples': samples,
            'node_id': node_id or None}


@router.get('/streams')
def streams(claims=Depends(OFFICER)):
    conn = get_conn()
    cams = [dict(r) for r in conn.execute('SELECT camera_id FROM cameras')]
    return {'streams': [{'camera_id': c['camera_id'], 'fps': 2,
                         'url': f"/api/v1/media/stream/{c['camera_id']}", 'active': True} for c in cams]}


@router.post('/controls/signal-mode')
async def signal_mode(body: dict, claims=Depends(COMMANDER)):
    mode = body.get('mode')
    if mode not in ('fixed', 'smart'):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=err('VALIDATION', 'mode phải là fixed|smart'))
    await broadcast('cmd.signal-mode', {'mode': mode}, to_roles=['station'])
    return {'ok': True, 'mode': mode}


@router.post('/controls/signal-override')
async def signal_override(body: dict, claims=Depends(COMMANDER)):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    duration = float(body.get('duration_s', 60))
    until_at = datetime.fromtimestamp(now.timestamp() + duration, timezone.utc).isoformat().replace('+00:00', 'Z')
    node_id = body.get('node_id')
    group = body.get('group')
    # `until_t_sim` là giây SIM; officer web không biết đồng hồ sim nên backend nhận từ trạm
    # (trường `until_t_sim` trong body nếu trạm đã biết) hoặc để trạm báo lại qua `station.metrics.overrides`.
    until_t_sim = body.get('until_t_sim')
    if group is None:
        OVERRIDES.pop(node_id, None)
    else:
        OVERRIDES[node_id] = {'node_id': node_id, 'group': group, 'by': claims['sub'],
                              'reason': body.get('reason', ''), 'until_t_sim': until_t_sim, 'until_at': until_at}
    await broadcast('cmd.signal-override', {'node_id': node_id, 'group': group,
                                            'duration_s': duration, 'by': claims['sub'], 'reason': body.get('reason', '')},
                    to_roles=['station'])
    return {'ok': True, 'until_t_sim': until_t_sim, 'until_at': until_at}


@router.get('/controls/state')
def controls_state(claims=Depends(OFFICER)):
    conn = get_conn()
    last = conn.execute('SELECT mode FROM kpi_samples ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1').fetchone()
    return {'mode': last['mode'] if last else 'fixed', 'overrides': list(OVERRIDES.values()), 'scenario': None}
```

```python
def test_lifecycle_m1_only_4_states(client):

    assert client.post(f'/api/v1/officer/violations/{vid}/needs-review', headers=oh, json={'note': 'x'}).status_code == 409
    assert client.post(f'/api/v1/officer/violations/{vid}/dismiss', headers=oh, json={'note': 'nhầm'}).status_code == 409
```

```python
import json

from starlette.websockets import WebSocketDisconnect


def test_ws_requires_auth_within_5s(client):
    with client.websocket_connect('/ws/v1') as ws:
        try:
            ws.receive_text()
        except WebSocketDisconnect:
            return
        raise AssertionError('phải đóng khi chưa auth')


def test_ws_auth_then_station_event_broadcast(client):
    # Trạm phát event → web công an nhận; trạm KHÔNG tự nhận lại event của mình.
    stoken = client.post('/api/v1/auth/login', json={'username': 'station-3d', 'password': 'SaiGon@2026'}).json()['access_token']
    otoken = client.post('/api/v1/auth/login', json={'username': 'officer1', 'password': 'SaiGon@2026'}).json()['access_token']
    with client.websocket_connect('/ws/v1') as officer:
        officer.send_text(json.dumps({'type': 'auth', 'token': otoken}))
        with client.websocket_connect('/ws/v1') as station:
            station.send_text(json.dumps({'type': 'auth', 'token': stoken}))
            station.send_text(json.dumps({'event': 'station.heartbeat', 'data': {'station_id': 'STATION-3D-01', 'fps': 60}}))
            msg = json.loads(officer.receive_text())
            assert msg['event'] == 'station.heartbeat'
            assert 'seq' in msg and 't' in msg


def test_ws_non_station_cannot_send_cmd(client):
    # Citizen/officer gửi `cmd.*` qua WS phải bị BỎ QUA (chỉ REST mới phát cmd.*).
    token = client.post('/api/v1/auth/login', json={'username': 'citizen1', 'password': 'SaiGon@2026'}).json()['access_token']
    with client.websocket_connect('/ws/v1') as ws:
        ws.send_text(json.dumps({'type': 'auth', 'token': token}))
        ws.send_text(json.dumps({'event': 'cmd.signal-override', 'data': {'node_id': 'HX-01', 'group': 0}}))
        ws.send_text(json.dumps({'event': 'station.heartbeat', 'data': {}}))
        # Không có phản hồi nào được broadcast lại cho chính kết nối này → đóng sạch.
        assert True
```

  - `GET /api/v1/media/{media_id}` — trả file JPEG/PNG + header `ETag`; không có → 404; **kiểm quyền**: `officer|commander|admin|station` xem mọi media, còn lại chỉ xem bằng chứng của vi phạm `confirmed+` (nếu không → 403). Id media sinh ngẫu nhiên khó đoán.
  - `GET /api/v1/media/stream/{camera_id}` — khung mới nhất JPEG (`?fps=2`); không có khung → 404 JSON.
  - `GET /api/v1/media/video/{camera_id}` — `media/cameras/<CAM-ID>.mp4` nếu có file, ngược lại 404.

`outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/routers/citizen.py`:

```python
import json

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException

from app.db import get_conn
from app.errors import err

router = APIRouter(prefix='/api/v1/public', tags=['public'])
# M1 chỉ có 4 trạng thái; công dân chỉ thấy vi phạm từ `confirmed` trở lên (ẩn `new`/`needs_review`/`dismissed`).
CITIZEN_STATUSES = ('confirmed', 'notified', 'paid', 'disputed')


@router.get('/lookup')
def lookup(plate: str, vehicle_class: str = 'car'):
    if not plate:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=422, detail=err('VALIDATION', 'Thiếu plate'))
    conn = get_conn()
    placeholders = ','.join('?' * len(CITIZEN_STATUSES))
    rows = conn.execute(f'SELECT * FROM violations WHERE plate=? AND status IN ({placeholders}) ORDER BY occurred_at DESC LIMIT 50',
                        (plate, *CITIZEN_STATUSES)).fetchall()
    items = []
    for r in rows:
        penalty = json.loads(r['penalty_json'] or '{}')
        verified = bool(penalty.get('verified'))
        ev = conn.execute('SELECT kind, media_id FROM evidence WHERE violation_id=?', (r['id'],)).fetchall()
        items.append({
            'id': r['id'], 'violation_code': r['violation_code'],
            'violation_label': 'Không chấp hành hiệu lệnh của đèn tín hiệu giao thông',
            'occurred_at': r['occurred_at'], 'node_label': r['node_label'], 'camera_id': r['camera_id'],
            'status': r['status'],
            # Chưa xác minh → KHÔNG lộ số tiền (null); web hiện "Mức phạt đang cập nhật…".
            'penalty': {'vnd_min': penalty.get('vnd_min') if verified else None,
                        'vnd_max': penalty.get('vnd_max') if verified else None,
                        'verified': verified},
            'evidence': [{'kind': e['kind'], 'url': f"/api/v1/media/{e['media_id']}"} for e in ev],
        })
    return {'items': items, 'total': len(items)}
```

`outputs/smart-traffic/backend/app/routers/media.py`:

```python
from pathlib import Path

from fastapi import APIRouter, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.responses import FileResponse

from app import config
from app.auth import decode_token
from app.db import get_conn
from app.errors import err
from app.routers.citizen import CITIZEN_STATUSES

router = APIRouter(prefix='/api/v1/media', tags=['media'])


@router.get('/{media_id}')
def get_media(media_id: str, request: Request):
    conn = get_conn()
    row = conn.execute('SELECT * FROM media_assets WHERE id=?', (media_id,)).fetchone()
    if not row or not Path(row['path']).exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=err('NOT_FOUND', 'Không có media'))
    # Phân quyền: officer/commander/admin/station xem mọi media; công dân/ẩn danh chỉ xem
    # bằng chứng của vi phạm `confirmed+` (M1) — id ngẫu nhiên nhưng KHÔNG đủ để bỏ qua kiểm quyền.
    role = None
    auth = request.headers.get('Authorization', '')
    if auth.startswith('Bearer '):
        try:
            role = decode_token(auth[7:]).get('role')
        except Exception:
            role = None
    if role not in ('officer', 'commander', 'admin', 'station'):
        placeholders = ','.join('?' * len(CITIZEN_STATUSES))
        linked = conn.execute(
            f'SELECT v.status AS status FROM evidence e JOIN violations v ON v.id=e.violation_id WHERE e.media_id=? AND v.status IN ({placeholders})',
            (media_id, *CITIZEN_STATUSES)).fetchone()
        if not linked:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=err('FORBIDDEN', 'Không đủ quyền xem media'))
    return FileResponse(row['path'], media_type=row['mime'], headers={'ETag': row['sha256']})


def _frame_path(camera_id: str):
    p = config.VAR_DIR / 'frames' / f'{camera_id}-latest.jpg'
    if not p.exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=err('NOT_FOUND', 'Không có khung hình'))
    return p


@router.get('/stream/{camera_id}')
def stream(camera_id: str, fps: int = 2):
    from app.routers.station import FRAMES
    if camera_id not in FRAMES:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=err('NOT_FOUND', 'Không có khung hình'))
    return FileResponse(_frame_path(camera_id), media_type='image/jpeg')


@router.get('/video/{camera_id}')
def video(camera_id: str):
    p = config.ROOT_DIR / 'media' / 'cameras' / f'{camera_id}.mp4'
    if not p.exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=err('NOT_FOUND', 'Không có video'))
    return FileResponse(p, media_type='video/mp4')
```

### Task 8: `app=smart` trong cảnh 3D (khóa khu, ẩn panel, chuyển hướng, ghi đè nhãn cấm)

**Files:**

- Modify: `outputs/hcmc-poc/webgpu-materials-24.html` (dòng 229 — nạp module theo mode)
- Modify: `outputs/hcmc-poc/webgpu-materials-24.js` (dòng 21 import, 735–745 gọi `createTraffic31`, 845 `history.replaceState`, chỗ render `materialStatus`)
- Test: `outputs/hcmc-poc/test/smart-app-32.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `buildSignalPlan32` (T1), `node_map.json` (T1), `createTraffic31` (T1 — options `signalPlan/breakerS`), `regionTrafficPayload('bac')`, `REGIONS_31` (`city-region-31.js?v=31a`).
- Produces (`smart-app-32.js`):
  - `export const SMART_APP_32_VERSION = 'smart-app-32a'`, `export const SMART_BREAKER_S = Infinity`.
  - `export const BANNED_STRINGS = ['giả lập', 'mô phỏng', 'minh họa', 'illustrative', 'simulated', 'demo', 'fake']`.
  - `export const SMART_LABELS_32` — bảng ghi đè nhãn (xem Bước 3).
  - `export function sanitizeStatusText32(text)` → xóa/cô lập mọi chuỗi cấm trước khi in DOM; trả về chuỗi sạch.
  - `export const HIDDEN_PANELS_32 = ['#story']` — selector panel cổ điển THẬT (giữ `#controls` để còn nút Flood Lab).
  - `export function applySmartLabels32(root)` → đếm số nhãn đã thay (`#floodState span` là selector thật).
  - `export function observeSmartLabels32(root)` → MutationObserver áp lại nhãn khi `flood-ui-25.js` ghi đè `#floodState`.
  - `export function lockRegions32(root)` → khóa mọi nút khu khác `bac`, ẩn `HIDDEN_PANELS_32`.
  - `export async function prepareSmart32({ graph })` → `{ signalPlan, nodeMap }` (dựng `buildSignalPlan32` với `node_map.json` fetch từ `../smart-traffic/backend/app/seed/node_map.json`).
  - **CHỮ KÝ CHUẨN (chốt từ T8, T9/T10/T11/T14 chỉ mở rộng qua đối tượng trả về):** `export function initSmartApp32({ scene, camera, renderer, traffic, status, root = document, mode = 'smart' })` → `app` có `{ controller, traffic, scene, camera, renderer, status, root, modules, registerSmartModule32(name, mod), dispose() }` — tạo `createSignalController32({net: traffic.net, sim: traffic.sim, mode:'smart'})`, `traffic.sim.attachController(controller)`, áp nhãn/khóa khu/ẩn panel. Module sau gọi `app.registerSmartModule32('signals'|'capture'|'transport'|'station-loop', mod)`.

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { BANNED_STRINGS, SMART_LABELS_32, sanitizeStatusText32, applySmartLabels32, lockRegions32, SMART_APP_32_VERSION } from '../smart-app-32.js?v=32a';

const fakeDom = () => {
  const mk = text => ({ textContent: text, setAttribute(k, v) { this[k] = v; }, disabled: false, hidden: false });
  const regionTitle = mk('Khu mô phỏng');
  const floodSpan = mk('Chạy mô phỏng 1D/2D');
  const floodStateSpan = mk('Không nạp được registry · mô phỏng đã khóa');
  const story = mk('panel'); story.id = 'story';
  const btnBac = mk('Phía bắc'); btnBac.dataset = { region: 'bac' };
  const btnAll = mk('Toàn bộ'); btnAll.dataset = { region: 'all' };
  const btnNam = mk('Phía nam'); btnNam.dataset = { region: 'nam' };
  const bySel = { '#regionTitle': regionTitle, '#runFloodScenario span': floodSpan, '#floodState span': floodStateSpan, '#floodState': floodStateSpan, '#story': story };
  return {
    regionTitle, floodSpan, floodStateSpan, story, btnBac, btnAll, btnNam,
    querySelector: sel => bySel[sel] || null,
    querySelectorAll: sel => sel === '[data-region]' ? [btnBac, btnAll, btnNam] : []
  };
};

test('sanitizeStatusText32 loại chuỗi cấm, giữ câu sạch', () => {
  assert.equal(SMART_APP_32_VERSION, 'smart-app-32a');
  assert.ok(BANNED_STRINGS.includes('mô phỏng'));
  const clean = sanitizeStatusText32('Khu vực · Chạy tính toán 1D/2D');
  assert.equal(clean, 'Khu vực · Chạy tính toán 1D/2D');
  const dirty = sanitizeStatusText32('giao thông minh họa (illustrative) trên đồ thị');
  for (const bad of BANNED_STRINGS) assert.ok(!dirty.toLowerCase().includes(bad), bad);
});

test('applySmartLabels32 ghi đè đúng 3 nhãn mục 9.3 (selector THẬT #floodState span)', () => {
  const dom = fakeDom();
  const n = applySmartLabels32(dom);
  assert.equal(n, 3);
  assert.equal(dom.regionTitle.textContent, 'Khu vực');
  assert.equal(dom.floodSpan.textContent, 'Chạy tính toán 1D/2D');
  assert.ok(!dom.floodStateSpan.textContent.includes('mô phỏng'));
  for (const item of SMART_LABELS_32) assert.ok(item.from && item.to);
});

test('lockRegions32: giữ bac, khóa mọi lựa chọn khác, ẩn panel cổ điển #story', () => {
  const dom = fakeDom();
  lockRegions32(dom);
  assert.equal(dom.btnBac.disabled, false);
  assert.equal(dom.btnAll.disabled, true);
  assert.equal(dom.btnNam.disabled, true);
  assert.equal(dom.btnNam['aria-disabled'], 'true');
  assert.equal(dom.story.hidden, true);
});
```

- [ ] **Bước 3: Cài đặt `outputs/hcmc-poc/smart-app-32.js`**

```js
// Vỏ app=smart — chỉ tải khi có ?app=smart. Không sửa file 31A; mọi ghi đè nhãn làm tại đây.
// CHỮ KÝ CHUẨN: initSmartApp32({ scene, camera, renderer, traffic, status, root, mode }).
// Các task sau (T9/T10/T11/T14) KHÔNG đổi chữ ký — chỉ mở rộng qua app.registerSmartModule32(name, mod).
import { buildSignalPlan32 } from './signal-plan-32.js?v=32a';
import { createSignalController32 } from './traffic-controller-32.js?v=32a';

export const SMART_APP_32_VERSION = 'smart-app-32a';
export const SMART_BREAKER_S = Infinity;
export const BANNED_STRINGS = ['giả lập', 'mô phỏng', 'minh họa', 'illustrative', 'simulated', 'demo', 'fake'];
// Selector panel cổ điển THẬT trong webgpu-materials-24.html (giữ #controls để còn nút Flood Lab, #floodLab).
export const HIDDEN_PANELS_32 = ['#story'];
export const SMART_LABELS_32 = [
  { selector: '#regionTitle', from: 'Khu mô phỏng', to: 'Khu vực' },
  { selector: '#runFloodScenario span', from: 'Chạy mô phỏng 1D/2D', to: 'Chạy tính toán 1D/2D' },
  { selector: '#floodState span', from: 'Không nạp được registry · mô phỏng đã khóa', to: 'Không nạp được registry · chế độ đã khóa' }
];

const escapeRe = s => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');

export function sanitizeStatusText32(text) {
  let out = String(text ?? '');
  for (const bad of BANNED_STRINGS) out = out.replace(new RegExp(escapeRe(bad), 'gi'), '');
  return out.replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ').replace(/\s+([,.:;])/g, '$1').trim();
}

export function applySmartLabels32(root) {
  let n = 0;
  for (const item of SMART_LABELS_32) {
    const el = root.querySelector(item.selector);
    if (el && el.textContent === item.from) { el.textContent = item.to; n++; }
    else if (el && BANNED_STRINGS.some(b => el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(b.toLowerCase()))) {
      el.textContent = sanitizeStatusText32(el.textContent); n++;
    }
  }
  return n;
}

// flood-ui-25.js ghi đè `#floodState` bằng innerHTML mỗi lần đổi kịch bản → áp lại nhãn sau khi nó ghi.
export function observeSmartLabels32(root) {
  const target = root.querySelector ? root.querySelector('#floodState') : null;
  if (!target || typeof MutationObserver === 'undefined') return null;
  const obs = new MutationObserver(() => applySmartLabels32(root));
  obs.observe(target, { childList: true, subtree: true, characterData: true });
  return obs;
}

export function lockRegions32(root) {
  for (const btn of root.querySelectorAll('[data-region]')) {
    const id = btn.dataset.region;
    if (id === 'bac') { btn.disabled = false; btn.setAttribute('aria-checked', 'true'); continue; }
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.setAttribute('aria-disabled', 'true');
    btn.title = 'Khu vực này tạm khóa';
  }
  for (const sel of HIDDEN_PANELS_32) {
    const el = root.querySelector(sel);
    if (el) el.hidden = true;
  }
}

export async function prepareSmart32({ graph }) {
  const res = await fetch('../smart-traffic/backend/app/seed/node_map.json');
  const nodeMap = await res.json();
  return { signalPlan: buildSignalPlan32({ graph, nodeMap }), nodeMap };
}

export function initSmartApp32({ scene, camera, renderer, traffic, status, root = document, mode = 'smart' }) {
  applySmartLabels32(root);
  lockRegions32(root);
  observeSmartLabels32(root);
  const controller = createSignalController32({ net: traffic.net, sim: traffic.sim, mode });
  traffic.sim.attachController(controller);
  const modules = {};
  const app = {
    controller, traffic, scene, camera, renderer, status, root, modules,
    registerSmartModule32(name, mod) { modules[name] = mod; return mod; },
    dispose() { for (const m of Object.values(modules)) if (m && typeof m.dispose === 'function') m.dispose(); }
  };
  return app;
}
```

### Task 9: Đèn 3D + đếm ngược + cột camera cụm Hàng Xanh

**Files:**
- Create: `outputs/hcmc-poc/traffic-signals-32.js`
- Modify: `outputs/hcmc-poc/smart-app-32.js` (trong `initSmartApp32` — dựng `createTrafficSignals32`, gọi `update` mỗi khung)
- Test: `outputs/hcmc-poc/test/traffic-signals-32.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `controller.stateOf(node, tSim)` (T1 — `SignalState`), `node_map.json` (6 nút HX), `cameras.json` (T3 — cột camera `CAM-HX-001/002`), THREE từ `three/webgpu`.
- Produces (`traffic-signals-32.js`):
  - `export const SIGNALS_32_VERSION = 'signals-32a'`.
  - `export function lightColorOf(phase)` → `'red'|'yellow'|'green'` (đèn sáng theo phase của nhóm).
  - `export function createTrafficSignals32({ scene, THREE, controller, nodeMap, cameras })` → `{ group, update(tSim), dispose() }` — mỗi nút: cột đèn (2 cụm 3 màu theo nhóm pha 0/1), bảng đếm ngược `countdown_s` (canvas texture), cột camera + nón FOV mờ tại vị trí camera. `THREE` **bắt buộc truyền** (không import tĩnh).
  - `export function installSignals32(app, { nodeMap, cameras, THREE })` → tạo `createTrafficSignals32` từ `app.scene/app.controller/app.traffic` (KHÔNG đổi chữ ký `initSmartApp32`), tự chạy `update` mỗi `requestAnimationFrame`, `app.registerSmartModule32('signals', signals)`, trả `signals`.

**Phụ thuộc:** Task 1, Task 3 (cameras.json), Task 8. **T9 → T10 → T11 chạy TUẦN TỰ** (cùng sửa `smart-app-32.js` và nối vào `initSmartApp32` chốt ở T8). T9 xong mới T10, T10 xong mới T11.

**Model đề xuất:** `mimo-v2.6-pro` (đèn 3D gắn cảnh)

- [ ] **Bước 3: Cài đặt `outputs/hcmc-poc/traffic-signals-32.js`**

```js
// Đèn giao thông 3D + đếm ngược + cột camera cho cụm Hàng Xanh (M1).
// KHÔNG import tĩnh 'three/webgpu' (Node không resolve được) — THREE truyền qua tham số.
export const SIGNALS_32_VERSION = 'signals-32a';
const PHASE_COLOR = { green: 0x2ecc71, yellow: 0xf1c40f, red: 0xe74c3c };
const IDLE_COLOR = 0x2a2f33;

export function lightColorOf(phase) {
  return phase === 'green' ? 'green' : phase === 'yellow' ? 'yellow' : 'red';
}

export function createTrafficSignals32({ scene, THREE, controller, nodeMap, cameras }) {
  if (!THREE) throw new TypeError('createTrafficSignals32 cần THREE (truyền từ app, không import tĩnh)');
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  group.name = 'traffic-signals-32';
  scene.add(group);
  const heads = [];   // {nodeIndex, groupIdx, lampMeshes:{green,yellow,red}, countdownMesh, texture, canvas}

  const makeLamp = (color, x, y) => {
    const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.28, 12, 12),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: IDLE_COLOR, emissive: IDLE_COLOR, emissiveIntensity: 0.2 }));
    mesh.position.set(x, y, 0);
    return mesh;
  };

  for (const node of nodeMap) {
    const pole = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.12, 0.16, 5.2, 10),
      new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x555c60, roughness: 0.7 }));
    pole.position.set(node.x + 2.2, 2.6, node.z + 2.2);
    group.add(pole);
    for (const g of [0, 1]) {
      const box = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(0.8, 2.1, 0.5),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x22262a, roughness: 0.8 }));
      box.position.set(node.x + 2.2 + g * 1.1, 4.6, node.z + 2.2);
      group.add(box);
      const lamps = {
        green: makeLamp(PHASE_COLOR.green, 0, 0.65),
        yellow: makeLamp(PHASE_COLOR.yellow, 0, 0),
        red: makeLamp(PHASE_COLOR.red, 0, -0.65)
      };
      for (const m of Object.values(lamps)) { box.add(m); }
      // Bảng đếm ngược: canvas 128×64 → texture.
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width = 128; canvas.height = 64;
      const texture = new THREE.CanvasTexture(canvas);
      const countdown = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(1.2, 0.6),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: texture, transparent: true }));
      countdown.position.set(node.x + 2.2 + g * 1.1, 3.2, node.z + 2.2);
      group.add(countdown);
      heads.push({ nodeIndex: node, groupIdx: g, lamps, countdown, canvas, texture });
    }
    // Cột camera + nón FOV (nếu node có camera).
    const cams = (cameras || []).filter(c => c.node_id === node.node_id);
    for (const cam of cams) {
      const column = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(0.1, 0.12, cam.height_m, 8),
        new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: 0x6b7276 }));
      column.position.set(cam.x, cam.height_m / 2, cam.z);
      group.add(column);
      const coneLen = cam.range_m;
      const cone = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(Math.tan((cam.fov_deg * Math.PI / 180) / 2) * coneLen, coneLen, 16, 1, true),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0x69a7ff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.12, side: THREE.DoubleSide, depthWrite: false }));
      const yaw = cam.yaw_deg * Math.PI / 180, pitch = cam.pitch_deg * Math.PI / 180;
      cone.position.set(cam.x + Math.cos(yaw) * coneLen / 2 * Math.cos(pitch), cam.height_m + Math.sin(pitch) * coneLen / 2, cam.z + Math.sin(yaw) * coneLen / 2 * Math.cos(pitch));
      cone.rotation.set(pitch - Math.PI / 2, 0, -yaw + Math.PI / 2);
      group.add(cone);
    }
  }

  const drawCountdown = (head, seconds) => {
    const ctx = head.canvas.getContext('2d');
    ctx.fillStyle = '#111417';
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, 128, 64);
    ctx.fillStyle = '#f4f7f8';
    ctx.font = 'bold 44px Arial';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText(String(Math.max(0, Math.ceil(seconds))), 64, 48);
    head.texture.needsUpdate = true;
  };

  return {
    group,
    update(tSim) {
      for (const head of heads) {
        const state = controller.stateOf(head.nodeIndex.node_id, tSim);   // head.nodeIndex là hàng nodeMap (có .node_id/.index)
        const phaseInfo = state.phases.find(p => p.group === head.groupIdx) || state.phases[0];
        const lit = lightColorOf(phaseInfo.phase);
        for (const [name, mesh] of Object.entries(head.lamps)) {
          const on = name === lit;
          mesh.material.color.setHex(on ? PHASE_COLOR[name] : IDLE_COLOR);
          mesh.material.emissive.setHex(on ? PHASE_COLOR[name] : IDLE_COLOR);
          mesh.material.emissiveIntensity = on ? 1.2 : 0.15;
        }
        drawCountdown(head, phaseInfo.countdown_s);
      }
    },
    dispose() {
      scene.remove(group);
    }
  };
}
```

Ghi chú hợp đồng `stateOf`: nhận **node_id (chuỗi)** hoặc **chỉ số nút**; `createTrafficSignals32` dùng `head.nodeIndex.node_id` (hàng nodeMap có `.node_id`, và `.index` do init tra). Test Node thuần chỉ import `lightColorOf` (không cần THREE).

Trong `smart-app-32.js`, **KHÔNG đổi chữ ký** `initSmartApp32` (đã chốt ở T8 `{ scene, camera, renderer, traffic, status }`). T9 thêm hàm cài đặt module, đăng ký qua đối tượng app trả về:

```js
// T9 — cài đèn 3D vào app smart (app = kết quả initSmartApp32 của T8).
export function installSignals32(app, { nodeMap = [], cameras = [], THREE = null }) {
  const traffic = app.traffic;
  const signals = createTrafficSignals32({
    scene: app.scene, THREE, controller: app.controller,
    nodeMap: nodeMap.map(m => ({ ...m, index: traffic.net.G.nodes.findIndex(n => n.id === m.osm_id) })),
    cameras
  });
  const tick = () => { signals.update(traffic.sim.counters.t); requestAnimationFrame(tick); };
  tick();
  app.registerSmartModule32('signals', signals);
  return signals;
}
```

(Trong `webgpu-materials-24.js` (nhánh smart, sau khi tạo `smartApp`), gọi:
```js
if (smartApp) {
  const { nodeMap } = await smartMod.prepareSmart32({ graph: window.ROAD_GRAPH_31 });
  const cameras = await fetch('../smart-traffic/backend/app/seed/cameras.json').then(r => r.json());
  smartMod.installSignals32(smartApp, { nodeMap, cameras, THREE });
}
```
)

Run: `node --test outputs/hcmc-poc/test/traffic-signals-32.test.mjs`
Kỳ vọng: 1/1 PASS.

### Task 10: Camera render-to-texture — ảnh bằng chứng + khung stream

**Files:**
- Create: `outputs/hcmc-poc/camera-capture-32.js`
- Modify: `outputs/hcmc-poc/smart-app-32.js` (tạo capture, chụp khi có vi phạm — nối với perception Task 11)
- Test: `outputs/hcmc-poc/test/camera-capture-32.test.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `cameras.json` (T3), `renderer/scene/THREE`, `api.snapshot()/api.poses()` (city-traffic-31.js), bản ghi vi phạm (T3).
- Produces (`camera-capture-32.js`):
  - `export const CAPTURE_32_VERSION = 'capture-32a'`.
  - `export function projectToCamera32(x, z, cam)` → `{distance_m, angle_rad}` (toạ độ XZ; `angle_rad` = lệch so với `yaw_deg`) — thuần toán, chạy Node. `export function projectToPixel32(x, z, cam, w, h)` → `{px, py, distance_m}` (chiếu xuống pixel ngang khung).
  - `export function createCameraCapture32({ renderer, scene, THREE, cameras })` → `{ captureFrame(cameraId) -> {jpeg, w, h} | null, captureEvidence(cameraId, vehicle, tSim, beforeFrame = null) -> {before, after, plate_crop}, latestFrame(cameraId), setStream(cameraId, on, fps) }`; `vehicle = {x, z}` là vị trí xe tại thời điểm vi phạm; `beforeFrame` là khung TRƯỚC vạch do vòng trạm giữ (nếu không truyền thì dùng buffer nội bộ).
  - Ảnh `before`/`after`: render-to-texture (`THREE.WebGLRenderTarget` 1280×720) từ camera 3D của `camera_id`; `before` chụp ở thời điểm sim TRƯỚC vạch, `after` ở thời điểm vi phạm; `plate_crop`: cắt ô 320×120 **theo vị trí chiếu của xe** (`projectToPixel32`), không phải ô cố định giữa khung.

**Phụ thuộc:** Task 8, **Task 9** (cùng lớp cảnh, TUẦN TỰ sau T9); Task 3 (cameras.json). Chạy song song được với Task 12 (không chung file), nhưng KHÔNG song song với T11.

**Model đề xuất:** `mimo-v2.6-pro` (render-to-texture)
