- [ ] **Bước 3: Cài đặt `outputs/hcmc-poc/camera-capture-32.js`**

```js
// Render-to-texture camera theo registry cameras.json: khung stream + ảnh bằng chứng.
export const CAPTURE_32_VERSION = 'capture-32a';

export function projectToCamera32(x, z, cam) {
  const dx = x - cam.x, dz = z - cam.z;
  const distance = Math.hypot(dx, dz);
  const toPoint = Math.atan2(dz, dx);
  const yaw = cam.yaw_deg * Math.PI / 180;
  let d = toPoint - yaw;
  while (d > Math.PI) d -= 2 * Math.PI;
  while (d < -Math.PI) d += 2 * Math.PI;
  return { distance_m: distance, angle_rad: d };
}

// Chiếu vị trí xe (x, z) xuống toạ độ pixel ngang trong khung (dùng cho `plate_crop` theo xe).
export function projectToPixel32(x, z, cam, w, h) {
  const { distance_m, angle_rad } = projectToCamera32(x, z, cam);
  const half = (cam.fov_deg * Math.PI / 180) / 2;
  const nx = Math.tan(Math.max(-half + 1e-4, Math.min(half - 1e-4, angle_rad))) / Math.tan(half);   // -1..1
  const px = (nx * 0.5 + 0.5) * w;
  return { px, py: h * 0.62, distance_m };
}

export function createCameraCapture32({ renderer, scene, THREE, cameras }) {
  const rigs = new Map();   // camera_id → {camera, target, latest}
  for (const cam of cameras) {
    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(cam.fov_deg, 16 / 9, 0.5, 3000);
    const yaw = cam.yaw_deg * Math.PI / 180, pitch = cam.pitch_deg * Math.PI / 180;
    camera.position.set(cam.x, cam.height_m, cam.z);
    camera.rotation.order = 'YXZ';
    // Hướng nhìn Three.js: -Z cục bộ. Cần `rotation.y = -yaw - π/2`, `rotation.x = pitch`
    // (pitch âm = cúi) để khớp perception `(cos yaw, sin yaw)` và `yaw_deg` trong cameras.json.
    camera.rotation.y = -yaw - Math.PI / 2;
    camera.rotation.x = pitch;
    const target = new THREE.WebGLRenderTarget(1280, 720);
    rigs.set(cam.camera_id, { cam, camera, target, latest: null });
  }
  const renderTo = rig => {
    renderer.setRenderTarget(rig.target);
    renderer.render(scene, rig.camera);
    renderer.setRenderTarget(null);
    const w = 1280, h = 720;
    const pixels = new Uint8Array(w * h * 4);
    const read = renderer.readRenderTargetPixelsAsync
      ? renderer.readRenderTargetPixelsAsync(rig.target, 0, 0, w, h, pixels).then(() => pixels)
      : Promise.resolve().then(() => { renderer.readRenderTargetPixels(rig.target, 0, 0, w, h, pixels); return pixels; });
    return read.then(buf => {
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      canvas.width = w; canvas.height = h;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
      const img = ctx.createImageData(w, h);
      // WebGL pixels đọc ngược chiều dọc → lật khi vẽ.
      for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) {
        const src = (h - 1 - y) * w * 4, dst = y * w * 4;
        img.data.set(buf.subarray(src, src + w * 4), dst);
      }
      ctx.putImageData(img, 0, 0);
      return { jpeg: canvas.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.8), w, h, canvas };
    });
  };
  return {
    captureFrame(cameraId) {
      const rig = rigs.get(cameraId);
      if (!rig) return null;
      return renderTo(rig).then(frame => {
        rig.buffer = rig.latest;   // giữ khung kế-trước làm `before` cho vi phạm
        rig.latest = { jpeg: frame.jpeg, seq: (rig.latest?.seq ?? 0) + 1, t: Date.now() };
        return rig.latest;
      });
    },
    captureEvidence(cameraId, vehicle, tSim, beforeFrame = null) {
      const rig = rigs.get(cameraId);
      if (!rig) return { before: null, after: null, plate_crop: null };
      const before = beforeFrame || rig.buffer;   // khung TRƯỚC vạch (vòng trạm giữ)
      return renderTo(rig).then(after => {
        // Cắt ô biển: hộp 320×120 theo vị trí CHIẾU của xe (không phải giữa khung).
        const { px } = projectToPixel32(vehicle.x, vehicle.z, rig.cam, after.w, after.h);
        const sx = Math.max(0, Math.min(after.w - 320, Math.round(px) - 160));
        const sy = Math.round(after.h * 0.62);
        const crop = document.createElement('canvas');
        crop.width = 320; crop.height = 120;
        const ctx = crop.getContext('2d');
        ctx.drawImage(after.canvas, sx, sy, 320, 120, 0, 0, 320, 120);
        const b = before || after;
        return {
          before: { jpeg: b.jpeg, w: b.w || after.w, h: b.h || after.h, kind: 'before' },
          after: { jpeg: after.jpeg, w: after.w, h: after.h, kind: 'after' },
          plate_crop: { jpeg: crop.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.9), w: 320, h: 120, kind: 'plate_crop' }
        };
      });
    },
    latestFrame: cameraId => rigs.get(cameraId)?.latest ?? null,
    setStream(cameraId, on, fps) {
      const rig = rigs.get(cameraId);
      if (!rig) return;
      if (on && !rig.timer) {
        rig.timer = setInterval(() => this.captureFrame(cameraId), 1000 / Math.max(1, fps || 2));
      } else if (!on && rig.timer) {
        clearInterval(rig.timer);
        rig.timer = null;
      }
    }
  };
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: hợp đồng REST/WS Task 6 (`/station/heartbeat|metrics|violations|violations/{id}/evidence|frames`, `ws://localhost:8900/ws/v1` với `{"type":"auth","token"}` trong 5 s); `controller.metrics(tSim)`/`setMode`/`overridePhase` (T1); bản ghi vi phạm + ảnh bằng chứng (T3, T10).
- Produces (`violation-transport-32.js`):
  - `export const TRANSPORT_32_VERSION = 'transport-32a'`, `export const QUEUE_MAX = 2000`, `export const HEARTBEAT_S = 15`, `BACKOFF_MIN_S = 1`, `BACKOFF_MAX_S = 30`.
  - `export function createQueue32({ store })` — hàng đợi FIFO **bất đồng bộ** (`store` inject: `{get,set}` trả Promise — IndexedDB hoặc Map trong test): `await push/shift/size/peekAll`, drop bản ghi cũ nhất khi vượt `QUEUE_MAX` (trả về số đã drop).
  - `export function createIndexedDbStore32(name, key)` — adapter IndexedDB thật (bất đồng bộ) cho store.

  - `export function installHud32(app, { transport })` — HUD A/B/KPI (không đổi chữ ký `initSmartApp32`); nối `transport.onCommand` → `controller.setMode/overridePhase`; vòng 5 s `transport.sendMetrics(controller.metrics(...))`; `app.registerSmartModule32('hud', panel)`.

```js
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { createQueue32, createTransport32, QUEUE_MAX, TRANSPORT_32_VERSION, HEARTBEAT_S } from '../violation-transport-32.js?v=32a';

// store bất đồng bộ (IndexedDB thật cũng bất đồng bộ).
const memStore = () => {
  const m = new Map();
  return { get: async k => m.get(k), set: async (k, v) => { m.set(k, v); } };
};

test('hàng đợi FIFO async, drop cũ nhất khi vượt QUEUE_MAX', async () => {
  assert.equal(TRANSPORT_32_VERSION, 'transport-32a');
  assert.equal(QUEUE_MAX, 2000);
  assert.equal(HEARTBEAT_S, 15);
  const q = createQueue32({ store: memStore() });
  for (let i = 0; i < 2005; i++) await q.push({ id: i });
  assert.equal(await q.size(), 2000);
  assert.equal((await q.shift()).id, 5);   // 5 bản ghi đầu đã bị drop
});

test('lỗi mạng → giữ trong hàng đợi; online → gửi lại và xoá', async () => {
  const sent = [];
  let fail = true;
  const fetchImpl = async (url, opts) => {
    if (fail) throw new Error('network');
    sent.push({ url, body: opts && opts.body });
    return { ok: true, status: 200, json: async () => ({ ok: true, id: 'VP-1' }) };
  };
  const q = createQueue32({ store: memStore() });
  const t = createTransport32({
    baseUrl: 'http://127.0.0.1:8900', wsUrl: '', token: 'x',
    stationId: 'STATION-3D-01', queue: q, fetchImpl, wsImpl: null, uptimeS: () => 0
  });
  await t.sendViolation({ violation_code: 'RED-LIGHT', vehicle_id: 'V-1' });
  assert.equal(await q.size(), 1);          // lỗi mạng → còn trong hàng đợi
  fail = false;
  await t.flush();
  assert.equal(await q.size(), 0);
  assert.equal(sent.length, 1);
  assert.match(sent[0].url, /\/api\/v1\/station\/violations$/);
});

test('409 = đã gửi (xoá); 422 → dead-letter; 5xx giữ lại', async () => {
  const makeFetch = statuses => {
    const fetchImpl = async url => ({ ok: statuses[0] >= 200 && statuses[0] < 300, status: statuses.shift(), json: async () => ({ ok: true }) });
    return fetchImpl;
  };
  const mk = async (status, id) => {
    const q = createQueue32({ store: memStore() });
    await q.push({ violation_code: 'RED-LIGHT', vehicle_id: id });
    const t = createTransport32({ baseUrl: 'http://x', token: 'x', stationId: 'S', queue: q, fetchImpl: makeFetch([status]), wsImpl: null, uptimeS: () => 0 });
    await t.flush();
    return { q, t };
  };
  assert.equal(await (await mk(409, 'V-409')).q.size(), 0);              // 409 xoá
  const dl = await mk(422, 'V-422');
  assert.equal(await dl.q.size(), 0);                                    // 4xx khác → dead-letter
  assert.equal(dl.t.deadLetterCount(), 1);
  const keep = await mk(500, 'V-500');
  keep.t.stop();
  assert.equal(await keep.q.size(), 1);                                  // 5xx giữ lại
});
```

- [ ] **Bước 3: Cài đặt `outputs/hcmc-poc/violation-transport-32.js`**

```js
// Client trạm: REST/WS + hàng đợi (adapter store BẤT ĐỒNG BỘ — IndexedDB), gửi lại khi có mạng (mục 8).
export const TRANSPORT_32_VERSION = 'transport-32a';
export const QUEUE_MAX = 2000;
export const HEARTBEAT_S = 15;
export const BACKOFF_MIN_S = 1;
export const BACKOFF_MAX_S = 30;

// store bất đồng bộ: { get(key) -> Promise<any>, set(key, value) -> Promise<void> }.
export function createQueue32({ store }) {
  let items = [];
  let loaded = false;
  const load = async () => { if (!loaded) { items = (await store.get('queue')) || []; loaded = true; } };
  const save = () => store.set('queue', items);
  return {
    async push(record) {
      await load();
      items.push(record);
      let dropped = 0;
      while (items.length > QUEUE_MAX) { items.shift(); dropped++; }
      await save();
      return dropped;
    },
    async shift() { await load(); const r = items.shift(); await save(); return r; },
    async size() { await load(); return items.length; },
    async peekAll() { await load(); return items.slice(); }
  };
}

// Adapter IndexedDB thật (trình duyệt) — dùng khi khởi tạo transport ở trạm 3D.
export function createIndexedDbStore32(name = 'smart-station-32', key = 'queue') {
  return {
    get(k) {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const req = indexedDB.open(name, 1);
        req.onupgradeneeded = () => req.result.createObjectStore('kv');
        req.onsuccess = () => {
          const tx = req.result.transaction('kv', 'readonly');
          const g = tx.objectStore('kv').get(k ?? key);
          g.onsuccess = () => resolve(g.result);
          g.onerror = () => reject(g.error);
        };
        req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
      });
    },
    set(k, v) {
      return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const req = indexedDB.open(name, 1);
        req.onupgradeneeded = () => req.result.createObjectStore('kv');
        req.onsuccess = () => {
          const tx = req.result.transaction('kv', 'readwrite');
          tx.objectStore('kv').put(v, k ?? key);
          tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
          tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);
        };
        req.onerror = () => reject(req.error);
      });
    }
  };
}

const dataUrlToBlob = dataUrl => {
  const [meta, b64] = String(dataUrl).split(',');
  const mime = (meta.match(/data:(.*?);/) || [])[1] || 'image/jpeg';
  const bin = atob(b64 || '');
  const arr = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) arr[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return new Blob([arr], { type: mime });
};

export function createTransport32({ baseUrl, wsUrl, token, stationId, queue, fetchImpl = fetch, wsImpl = null, uptimeS = () => 0 }) {
  let ws = null, backoffS = BACKOFF_MIN_S, stopped = false, heartbeatTimer = null, retryTimer = null, flushing = null;
  const listeners = [];
  const deadLetter = [];
  let online = true;
  const post = (path, body) => fetchImpl(baseUrl + path, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` },
    body: JSON.stringify(body)
  });
  const scheduleRetry = () => {
    if (stopped) return;
    clearTimeout(retryTimer);
    retryTimer = setTimeout(() => { flush(); }, backoffS * 1000);   // backoff setTimeout đệ quy 1→30 s
  };
  const doFlush = async () => {
    while (!stopped && (await queue.size()) > 0) {
      const [rec] = await queue.peekAll();
      let res;
      try { res = await post('/api/v1/station/violations', rec); }
      catch (e) { online = false; backoffS = Math.min(BACKOFF_MAX_S, backoffS * 2); scheduleRetry(); return; }
      if (res.ok) { await queue.shift(); backoffS = BACKOFF_MIN_S; online = true; continue; }
      if (res.status === 409) { await queue.shift(); continue; }                       // đã gửi
      if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 429 || res.status >= 500) {              // giữ lại để thử lại
        online = false; backoffS = Math.min(BACKOFF_MAX_S, backoffS * 2); scheduleRetry(); return;
      }
      deadLetter.push({ record: rec, status: res.status });                            // 4xx khác → dead-letter
      await queue.shift();
    }
    online = true;
  };
  // Tuần tự hoá flush: chỉ một vòng chạy tại một thời điểm (interval/backoff/sendViolation không shift nhầm).
  const flush = () => { if (!flushing) flushing = doFlush().finally(() => { flushing = null; }); return flushing; };
  const sendViolation = async record => {
    try {
      const res = await post('/api/v1/station/violations', record);
      if (res.ok) { online = true; backoffS = BACKOFF_MIN_S; return res.json ? await res.json() : { ok: true }; }
      if (res.status === 409) return { duplicate: true };
      if (res.status === 401 || res.status === 429 || res.status >= 500) { await queue.push(record); online = false; backoffS = Math.min(BACKOFF_MAX_S, backoffS * 2); scheduleRetry(); }
      else deadLetter.push({ record, status: res.status });
      return null;
    } catch (e) {
      await queue.push(record); online = false; backoffS = Math.min(BACKOFF_MAX_S, backoffS * 2); scheduleRetry(); return null;
    }
  };
  const sendEvidence = async (violationId, kind, dataUrl) => {
    const form = new FormData();
    form.append('kind', kind);
    form.append('file', dataUrlToBlob(dataUrl), `${kind}.jpg`);
    // KHÔNG đặt Content-Type: để fetch tự thêm boundary multipart.
    const res = await fetchImpl(baseUrl + `/api/v1/station/violations/${violationId}/evidence`, {
      method: 'POST', headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` }, body: form
    });
    return res.ok && res.json ? await res.json() : null;
  };
  const sendMetrics = payload => post('/api/v1/station/metrics', payload).catch(() => {});
  const sendFrame = (cameraId, seq, jpeg_base64, w, h) => post('/api/v1/station/frames', { camera_id: cameraId, seq, t: new Date().toISOString(), jpeg_base64, w, h }).catch(() => {});
  const start = () => {
    stopped = false;
    if (wsImpl && wsUrl) {
      ws = new wsImpl(wsUrl);
      ws.onopen = () => ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'auth', token }));
      ws.onmessage = ev => { try { const msg = JSON.parse(ev.data); for (const fn of listeners) fn(msg); } catch (e) { /* bỏ qua */ } };
    }
    const beat = async () => {
      if (stopped) return;
      try {
        await post('/api/v1/station/heartbeat', {
          station_id: stationId, app_version: TRANSPORT_32_VERSION,
          t: new Date().toISOString(), uptime_s: Math.floor(uptimeS()), fps: 0, webgpu: true
        });
      } catch (e) { /* thử lại nhịp sau */ }
      heartbeatTimer = setTimeout(beat, HEARTBEAT_S * 1000);   // heartbeat 15 s
    };
    beat();
    flush();
  };
  const stop = () => { stopped = true; clearTimeout(heartbeatTimer); clearTimeout(retryTimer); if (ws) ws.close(); };
  return {
    start, stop, flush, sendViolation, sendEvidence, sendMetrics, sendFrame,
    onCommand: fn => listeners.push(fn),
    connectionState: () => (online ? 'online' : 'offline'),
    deadLetterCount: () => deadLetter.length
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Bước 4: Viết HUD A/B/KPI + nối `onCommand`/`sendMetrics` — sửa `outputs/hcmc-poc/smart-app-32.js`**

Thêm vào cuối file (KHÔNG đổi chữ ký `initSmartApp32`; cài qua `app.registerSmartModule32`):

```js
export function installHud32(app, { transport = null } = {}) {
  const root = app.root || document;
  const controller = app.controller, sim = app.traffic.sim;
  const panel = document.createElement('aside');
  panel.id = 'smartHud32';
  panel.innerHTML = '<b>Trung tâm điều hành giao thông khu Bắc</b>'
    + '<div><span>KPI chờ TB: <b id="kpiWait32">–</b> s · Hàng chờ max: <b id="kpiQueue32">–</b> · Lưu lượng: <b id="kpiFlow32">–</b>/phút</span></div>'
    + '<div><button type="button" id="modeFixed32">Đèn cố định</button> <button type="button" id="modeSmart32">Thông minh</button></div>';
  (root.body || document.body).append(panel);
  panel.querySelector('#modeFixed32').onclick = () => controller.setMode('fixed');
  panel.querySelector('#modeSmart32').onclick = () => controller.setMode('smart');
  const tick = () => {
    const m = controller.metrics(sim ? sim.counters.t : 0);
    panel.querySelector('#kpiWait32').textContent = String(m.wait_avg_s);
    panel.querySelector('#kpiQueue32').textContent = String(m.queue_max);
    panel.querySelector('#kpiFlow32').textContent = String(m.flow_per_min);
    setTimeout(tick, 1000);
  };
  tick();
  // Nối lệnh backend → controller (cmd.signal-mode / cmd.signal-override từ REST officer/commander).
  if (transport) {
    transport.onCommand(msg => {
      const d = msg && msg.data || {};
      if (msg.event === 'cmd.signal-mode' && d.mode) controller.setMode(d.mode);
      if (msg.event === 'cmd.signal-override') controller.overridePhase(d.node_id, d.group, d.duration_s || 60, d.by || 'command', d.reason || '');
      if (msg.event === 'cmd.reload-seed') location.reload();
    });
    // Gửi metrics định kỳ 5 s để `kpi_samples` không rỗng (KPI web công an hiển thị thật).
    const metricsTick = () => {
      const m = controller.metrics(sim ? sim.counters.t : 0);
      transport.sendMetrics({
        station_id: 'STATION-3D-01', t: new Date().toISOString(), t_sim: sim ? sim.counters.t : 0,
        fps: 0, draw_calls: app.traffic && app.traffic.meshes && app.traffic.meshes.length || 0,
        kpi: { wait_avg_s: m.wait_avg_s, queue_max: m.queue_max, flow_per_min: m.flow_per_min },
        signal_mode: controller.getMode(),
        overrides: m.overrides.map(o => ({ node_id: o.node_id, group: o.group, until_t_sim: o.until_t_sim, until_at: o.until_at }))
      });
      setTimeout(metricsTick, 5000);
    };
    metricsTick();
  }
  return app.registerSmartModule32('hud', panel);
}
```

- [ ] **Bước 2: Viết `src/lib/api.ts`, `src/lib/ws.ts`, `src/lib/format.ts`**

`src/lib/api.ts`:

```ts
const BASE = (import.meta as any).env?.VITE_API_BASE || 'http://127.0.0.1:8900/api/v1';

export function authHeaders(): Record<string, string> {
  const token = localStorage.getItem('smart_token');
  return token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {};
}

export async function login(username: string, password: string) {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/auth/login`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ username, password })
  });
  const body = await res.json();
  if (body.access_token) localStorage.setItem('smart_token', body.access_token);
  return body;
}

export async function listViolations(query: Record<string, string>) {
  const qs = new URLSearchParams(query).toString();
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/officer/violations?${qs}`, { headers: authHeaders() });
  return res.json();
}

export async function getViolation(id: string) {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/officer/violations/${id}`, { headers: authHeaders() });
  return res.json();
}

export async function review(id: string, action: 'confirm' | 'dismiss' | 'needs-review', note: string) {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/officer/violations/${id}/${action}`, {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...authHeaders() },
    body: JSON.stringify({ note })
  });
  return res.json();
}

export async function getKpi() {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/officer/kpi`, { headers: authHeaders() });
  return res.json();
}

export async function getStreams() {
  const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/officer/streams`, { headers: authHeaders() });
  return res.json();
}

export const streamUrl = (cameraId: string) => `${BASE}/media/stream/${cameraId}`;
```

`src/lib/ws.ts`:

```ts
// URL WS suy từ VITE_API_BASE (không hardcode cổng 8900 — agent chạy song song tự đổi cổng).
const API_BASE = (import.meta as any).env?.VITE_API_BASE || 'http://127.0.0.1:8900/api/v1';
const WS_URL = (import.meta as any).env?.VITE_WS_URL
  || API_BASE.replace(/^http/, 'ws').replace(/\/api\/v1$/, '/ws/v1');

export function connectSmartWs(token: string, onEvent: (msg: any) => void) {
  let ws: WebSocket | null = null;
  let backoff = 1000;
  let closed = false;   // cờ dừng: không reconnect sau khi unmount
  const open = () => {
    if (closed) return;
    ws = new WebSocket(WS_URL);
    ws.onopen = () => {
      backoff = 1000;
      ws?.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'auth', token }));
    };
    ws.onmessage = ev => {
      try { onEvent(JSON.parse(ev.data)); } catch { /* bỏ qua tin rác */ }
    };
    ws.onclose = () => {
      if (closed) return;
      setTimeout(open, backoff);
      backoff = Math.min(30000, backoff * 2);
    };
  };
  open();
  return () => { closed = true; ws?.close(); };
}
```

`src/lib/format.ts`:

```ts
export function formatVnd(n: number): string {
  return n.toLocaleString('vi-VN') + ' đ';
}

export function formatPenalty(p: { vnd_min?: number; vnd_max?: number; verified?: boolean }): string {
  if (!p || !p.verified) return 'Mức phạt đang cập nhật theo NĐ 168/2024/NĐ-CP';
  return `${formatVnd(p.vnd_min ?? 0)} – ${formatVnd(p.vnd_max ?? 0)}`;
}
```

`App.tsx` (đầy đủ cho M1: đăng nhập, danh sách sống, duyệt/gỡ, 1 ô stream, KPI):

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { authHeaders, getKpi, listViolations, login, review, streamUrl } from '../lib/api';
import { connectSmartWs } from '../lib/ws';
import { formatPenalty } from '../lib/format';

export function App() {
  const [token, setToken] = useState(localStorage.getItem('smart_token'));
  const [user, setUser] = useState('');
  const [pw, setPw] = useState('');
  const [items, setItems] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [kpi, setKpi] = useState<any>({});
  const [err, setErr] = useState('');

  const refresh = async () => {
    // Danh sách sống: cả `new` lẫn `needs_review` (không chỉ `new`).
    const body = await listViolations({ page: '1', page_size: '50' });
    setItems((body.items || []).filter((v: any) => v.status === 'new' || v.status === 'needs_review'));
    setKpi(await getKpi());
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!token) return;
    refresh();
    const stop = connectSmartWs(token, msg => {
      if (['violation.created', 'violation.updated', 'kpi.update'].includes(msg.event)) refresh();
    });
    const timer = setInterval(refresh, 10000);
    return () => { stop(); clearInterval(timer); };
  }, [token]);

  if (!token) {
    return (
      <form onSubmit={async e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const body = await login(user, pw);
        if (body.access_token) setToken(body.access_token);
        else setErr('Sai tài khoản hoặc mật khẩu');
      }}>
        <h1>SaiGon SMTF · Trung tâm điều hành giao thông khu Bắc</h1>
        <input value={user} onChange={e => setUser(e.target.value)} placeholder="Tài khoản" />
        <input type="password" value={pw} onChange={e => setPw(e.target.value)} placeholder="Mật khẩu" />
        <button type="submit">Đăng nhập</button>
        <p>{err}</p>
      </form>
    );
  }

  return (
    <main>
      <h1>SaiGon SMTF · Trung tâm điều hành giao thông khu Bắc</h1>
      <section aria-label="KPI">
        <span>Chờ TB: <b>{kpi.wait_avg_s ?? '–'}</b> s</span>
        <span>Hàng chờ max: <b>{kpi.queue_max ?? '–'}</b></span>
        <span>Lưu lượng: <b>{kpi.flow_per_min ?? '–'}</b>/phút</span>
        <span>Chế độ: <b>{kpi.mode ?? '–'}</b></span>
      </section>
      <section aria-label="Stream">
        <img src={streamUrl('CAM-HX-001')} alt="Camera Hàng Xanh" width={480} />
      </section>
      <section aria-label="Danh sách vi phạm">
        <table>
          <thead><tr><th>Mã</th><th>Biển</th><th>Lỗi</th><th>Thời gian</th><th>Nút</th><th>Mức phạt</th><th>Thao tác</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>
            {items.map(v => (
              <tr key={v.id}>
                <td>{v.id}</td><td>{v.plate}</td><td>{v.violation_code}</td>
                <td>{v.occurred_at}</td><td>{v.node_id}</td>
                <td>{formatPenalty(v.penalty ?? { verified: false })}</td>
                <td>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => review(v.id, 'confirm', 'xác nhận')}>Xác nhận</button>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => review(v.id, 'dismiss', 'gỡ')}>Gỡ</button>
                  <button type="button" onClick={() => review(v.id, 'needs-review', 'cần xác minh')}>Cần xác minh</button>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
```

(`officer.css` tối giản: bảng vi phạm, hai nút màu; **không** chứa chuỗi cấm.)

- [ ] **Bước 1: Viết `src/entries/citizen/main.tsx` + `App.tsx`**

`main.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { App } from './App';
import './citizen.css';

createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(<App />);
```

`App.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { formatPenalty } from '../../lib/format';

const BASE = (import.meta as any).env?.VITE_API_BASE || 'http://127.0.0.1:8900/api/v1';

export function App() {
  const [plate, setPlate] = useState('');
  const [vehicleClass, setVehicleClass] = useState('car');
  const [items, setItems] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('');

  const lookup = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setMsg('Đang tra cứu…');
    const res = await fetch(`${BASE}/public/lookup?plate=${encodeURIComponent(plate)}&vehicle_class=${vehicleClass}`);
    const body = await res.json();
    setItems(body.items || []);
    setMsg(body.total ? `${body.total} kết quả` : 'Không tìm thấy bản ghi nào');
  };

  return (
    <main>
      <h1>Tra cứu vi phạm giao thông</h1>
      <form onSubmit={lookup}>
        <input value={plate} onChange={e => setPlate(e.target.value)} placeholder="Biển số, ví dụ 51K-123.45" required />
        <select value={vehicleClass} onChange={e => setVehicleClass(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="car">Ô tô</option>
          <option value="motorbike">Xe máy</option>
        </select>
        <button type="submit">Tra cứu</button>
      </form>
      <p>{msg}</p>
      <ul>
        {items.map(v => (
          <li key={v.id}>
            <b>{v.violation_code}</b> — {v.occurred_at} — {v.node_label} — {v.status}
            <div>{formatPenalty(v.penalty)}</div>
            <div>
              {v.evidence.map((e: any) => <img key={e.url} src={BASE.replace('/api/v1', '') + e.url} alt={e.kind} width={160} />)}
            </div>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </main>
  );
}
```

(`citizen.css` tối giản; không chuỗi cấm.)

### Task 14: `run-smart` + e2e Playwright toàn luồng + quét chuỗi cấm + đo FPS

**Files:**
- Create: `scripts/run-smart.ps1`, `scripts/run-smart.sh`

- Modify: `outputs/hcmc-poc/smart-app-32.js` (vòng lặp trạm: perception 0,5 s → capture bằng chứng → transport; truyền `redRun` tất định cho controller)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: mọi API Task 5–7, `createPerception32` (T3), `createCameraCapture32` (T10), `createTransport32` (T11), `plateFor32`, controller (T1), Playwright harness (`PW_MODULE`, `PW_CHROME`) kiểu `qa-region-31.cjs`.
- Produces:

Bản `scripts/run-smart.ps1` viết ĐỦ MÃ tương đương (UTF-8 **có BOM**, `Start-Process`, `-LiteralPath`).

- [ ] **Bước 1: Nối vòng lặp trạm trong `outputs/hcmc-poc/smart-app-32.js`**

Controller tạo với `redRun` tất định (mục 7.2). `initSmartApp32` nhận `redRun` (không đổi phần còn lại của chữ ký):

```js
export function makeRedRun32(controller, seed = 31031, rate = 0.03) {
  return (i, node, st, tSim) => {
    const cycle = controller.cycleIdOf(node, tSim);
    return ((hashU32((seed ^ (i + 1) ^ cycle) >>> 0) >>> 0) / 4294967296) < rate;
  };
}
```

`startStationLoop32` — quét 0,5 s → `sendViolation` lấy **`id` từ phản hồi server** rồi gửi bằng chứng **multipart** theo id đó; khung `before` lấy từ khung đã chụp trước vạch (`capture.latestFrame`):

```js
export async function startStationLoop32(app, { perception, capture, transport, cameras = [], seed = 31031 }) {
  let running = true;
  const tick = async () => {
    if (!running) return;
    const sim = app.traffic.sim;
    const tSim = sim.counters.t;
    // Giữ khung KẾ-TRƯỚC của mỗi camera làm `before` cho vi phạm sắp tới.
    for (const cam of cameras) if (capture.captureFrame) capture.captureFrame(cam.camera_id);
    perception.scan(tSim);
    for (const rec of perception.drainViolations()) {
      const before = capture.latestFrame(rec.camera_id);
      const sent = await transport.sendViolation(rec);      // {id} khi online; null khi đã xếp hàng (offline)
      const violationId = sent && sent.id ? sent.id : null;
      if (!violationId) continue;                            // offline: flush sau gửi lại; chưa có id nên chưa gắn bằng chứng
      const pose = vehiclePose32(app.traffic, rec);          // {x, z} vị trí xe lúc vi phạm
      const evid = await capture.captureEvidence(rec.camera_id, pose, tSim, before);
      for (const part of [evid.before, evid.after, evid.plate_crop]) {
        if (part && part.jpeg) await transport.sendEvidence(violationId, part.kind, part.jpeg);
      }
    }
    setTimeout(tick, 500);
  };
  tick();
  const loop = { stop: () => { running = false; } };
  app.registerSmartModule32('station-loop', loop);
  return loop;
}

function vehiclePose32(traffic, rec) {
  const sim = traffic.sim, net = traffic.net;
  const i = Number(String(rec.vehicle_id).replace(/\D/g, '')) - 1;
  const l = sim.lane[i], e = net.lanes.edge[l], c = net.E[e].c, n = c.length / 2;
  const k = sim.s[i] / Math.max(0.01, net.lanes.length[l]);
  return { x: c[0] + (c[n * 2 - 2] - c[0]) * k, z: c[1] + (c[n * 2 - 1] - c[1]) * k };
}
```

Trong `webgpu-materials-24.js` nhánh smart (sau `installSignals32`): fetch `cameras.json`, lấy token trạm bằng đăng nhập `station-3d` (lưu `localStorage.smart_station_token`), tạo perception/capture/transport rồi gắn:

```js
if (smartApp) {
  const cameras = await fetch('../smart-traffic/backend/app/seed/cameras.json').then(r => r.json());
  const stoken = await ensureStationToken32();               // POST /auth/login station-3d → localStorage.smart_station_token
  const perception = createPerception32({ net: traffic.net, sim: traffic.sim, controller: smartApp.controller, nodeMap, cameras, seed: 31031 });
  const capture = createCameraCapture32({ renderer, scene, THREE, cameras });
  const transport = createTransport32({
    baseUrl: VITE_API_BASE || `http://127.0.0.1:${SMART_BACKEND_PORT || 8900}`,
    wsUrl: (VITE_API_BASE || 'http://127.0.0.1:8900').replace(/^http/, 'ws') + '/ws/v1',
    token: stoken, stationId: 'STATION-3D-01',
    queue: createQueue32({ store: createIndexedDbStore32() }),
    uptimeS: () => performance.now() / 1000
  });
  transport.start();
  smartApp.registerSmartModule32('capture', capture);
  smartApp.registerSmartModule32('transport', transport);
  installHud32(smartApp, { transport });
  startStationLoop32(smartApp, { perception, capture, transport, cameras, seed: 31031 });
}
```

(import `hashU32` từ `./city-traffic-sim-31.js?v=32a`; `createPerception32`/`VIOLATION_RATE` từ `./violation-perception-32.js?v=32a`; `createCameraCapture32` từ `./camera-capture-32.js?v=32a`; `createTransport32/createQueue32/createIndexedDbStore32` từ `./violation-transport-32.js?v=32a`; `installHud32`/`startStationLoop32` cùng file `smart-app-32.js`.)

- [ ] **Bước 2: Viết `scripts/run-smart.sh`**

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Chạy cả hệ Smart Traffic khu Bắc. nohup cho tiến trình nền; --stop để tắt.
set -u
ROOT="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"
LOGS="$ROOT/outputs/smart-traffic/var/logs"
mkdir -p "$LOGS"

stop_all() {
  for name in static backend web; do
    if [ -f "$LOGS/$name.pid" ]; then
      kill "$(cat "$LOGS/$name.pid")" 2>/dev/null || true
      rm -f "$LOGS/$name.pid"
    fi
  done
  echo "Đã gửi lệnh tắt (static, backend, web)."
}

if [ "${1:-}" = "--stop" ]; then stop_all; exit 0; fi

command -v python >/dev/null || { echo "Thiếu python"; exit 1; }
command -v node >/dev/null || { echo "Thiếu node"; exit 1; }

PORT=8821 nohup python "$ROOT/outputs/hcmc-poc/serve.py" > "$LOGS/static.log" 2>&1 < /dev/null &
echo $! > "$LOGS/static.pid"

if [ ! -d "$ROOT/outputs/smart-traffic/backend/.venv" ]; then
  python -m venv "$ROOT/outputs/smart-traffic/backend/.venv"
  "$ROOT/outputs/smart-traffic/backend/.venv/Scripts/python" -m pip install -r "$ROOT/outputs/smart-traffic/backend/requirements.txt"
fi
(cd "$ROOT/outputs/smart-traffic/backend" && SMART_BACKEND_PORT="${SMART_BACKEND_PORT:-8900}" nohup ./.venv/Scripts/python -m uvicorn app.main:app --host 127.0.0.1 --port "${SMART_BACKEND_PORT:-8900}" > "$LOGS/backend.log" 2>&1 < /dev/null & echo $! > "$LOGS/backend.pid")

(cd "$ROOT/outputs/smart-traffic/web" && nohup npm run dev > "$LOGS/web.log" 2>&1 < /dev/null & echo $! > "$LOGS/web.pid")

echo "Static 3D:  http://127.0.0.1:8821/hcmc-poc/index.html?app=smart&v=32a"
echo "Backend:    http://127.0.0.1:8900/docs"
echo "Web dev:    http://127.0.0.1:5173/index-officer.html"
```

Đối ứng `scripts/run-smart.ps1` — viết ĐỦ MÃ (file lưu UTF-8 **có BOM**):

```powershell
# Chạy cả hệ Smart Traffic khu Bắc trên Windows. Tương đương run-smart.sh; --stop để tắt.
param([switch]$Stop)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
$Root = Split-Path -Parent (Split-Path -Parent $MyInvocation.MyCommand.Path)
$Logs = Join-Path $Root 'outputs/smart-traffic/var/logs'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $Logs | Out-Null
$BackendPort = if ($env:SMART_BACKEND_PORT) { $env:SMART_BACKEND_PORT } else { '8900' }

function Stop-All {
  foreach ($name in @('static', 'backend', 'web')) {
    $pidFile = Join-Path $Logs "$name.pid"
    if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $pidFile) {
      $procId = Get-Content -LiteralPath $pidFile
      Stop-Process -Id $procId -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
      Remove-Item -LiteralPath $pidFile -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    }
  }
  Write-Output 'Đã gửi lệnh tắt (static, backend, web).'
}

if ($Stop) { Stop-All; exit 0 }

$env:PORT = '8821'
$static = Start-Process -FilePath 'python' -ArgumentList (Join-Path $Root 'outputs/hcmc-poc/serve.py') `
  -WorkingDirectory $Root -NoNewWindow -PassThru `
  -RedirectStandardOutput (Join-Path $Logs 'static.log') -RedirectStandardError (Join-Path $Logs 'static.err.log')
$static.Id | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Logs 'static.pid')

$venv = Join-Path $Root 'outputs/smart-traffic/backend/.venv'
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $venv)) {
  python -m venv $venv
  & (Join-Path $venv 'Scripts/python.exe') -m pip install -r (Join-Path $Root 'outputs/smart-traffic/backend/requirements.txt')
}
$backend = Start-Process -FilePath (Join-Path $venv 'Scripts/python.exe') `
  -ArgumentList @('-m', 'uvicorn', 'app.main:app', '--host', '127.0.0.1', '--port', $BackendPort) `
  -WorkingDirectory (Join-Path $Root 'outputs/smart-traffic/backend') -NoNewWindow -PassThru `
  -RedirectStandardOutput (Join-Path $Logs 'backend.log') -RedirectStandardError (Join-Path $Logs 'backend.err.log')
$backend.Id | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Logs 'backend.pid')

$web = Start-Process -FilePath 'npm' -ArgumentList @('run', 'dev') `
  -WorkingDirectory (Join-Path $Root 'outputs/smart-traffic/web') -NoNewWindow -PassThru `
  -RedirectStandardOutput (Join-Path $Logs 'web.log') -RedirectStandardError (Join-Path $Logs 'web.err.log')
$web.Id | Set-Content -LiteralPath (Join-Path $Logs 'web.pid')

Write-Output 'Static 3D:  http://127.0.0.1:8821/hcmc-poc/index.html?app=smart&v=32a'
Write-Output "Backend:    http://127.0.0.1:$BackendPort/docs"
Write-Output 'Web dev:    http://127.0.0.1:5173/index-officer.html'
```

- [ ] **Bước 3: Viết `outputs/hcmc-poc/scripts/qa-smart-32.cjs`**

```js
/*
 * QA M1 — app smart Hàng Xanh: redirect, khóa khu, chuỗi cấm, luồng vi phạm → tra cứu, FPS (A3).
 * Chạy: PORT=8821 node outputs/hcmc-poc/scripts/qa-smart-32.cjs            → kiểm 1–4 (headless)
 *       PORT=8821 node outputs/hcmc-poc/scripts/qa-smart-32.cjs --fps      → đo FPS headed (A3, máy GPU)
 */
const assert = require('assert');
const PW = process.env.PW_MODULE || 'C:/Users/Admin/AppData/Roaming/npm/node_modules/@playwright/mcp/node_modules/playwright';
const CHROME = process.env.PW_CHROME || 'C:/Users/Admin/AppData/Local/ms-playwright/chromium-1243/chrome-win64/chrome.exe';
const BASE_3D = process.env.CITY_URL || `http://127.0.0.1:${process.env.PORT || 8821}/hcmc-poc`;
const API = process.env.SMART_API || `http://127.0.0.1:${process.env.SMART_BACKEND_PORT || 8900}/api/v1`;
const BANNED = ['giả lập', 'mô phỏng', 'minh họa', 'illustrative', 'simulated', 'demo', 'fake'];

const checks = [], failures = [];
const check = (name, fn) => Promise.resolve().then(fn).then(() => checks.push(name)).catch(error => failures.push({ name, error: String(error?.message || error).slice(0, 500) }));
const sleep = ms => new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, ms));

async function scanBanned(page) {
  return page.evaluate(banned => {
    const texts = [];
    const walk = document.documentElement;
    texts.push((walk.textContent || '').toLowerCase());
    for (const el of document.querySelectorAll('[aria-label],[title],[placeholder],[alt]')) {
      for (const attr of ['aria-label', 'title', 'placeholder', 'alt']) {
        const v = el.getAttribute(attr);
        if (v) texts.push(v.toLowerCase());
      }
    }
    const joined = texts.join(' ');
    return banned.filter(b => joined.includes(b));
  }, BANNED);
}

(async () => {
  const { chromium } = require(PW);
  const browser = await chromium.launch({ executablePath: CHROME, headless: !process.argv.includes('--fps') });
  const page = await browser.newPage();

  await check('A2 + redirect: index.html?app=smart&v=32a → webgpu-materials-24.html?v=32a&app=smart&region=bac', async () => {
    await page.goto(`${BASE_3D}/index.html?app=smart&v=32a`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
    await sleep(3000);
    const url = page.url();
    assert(url.includes('webgpu-materials-24.html'), url);
    assert(url.includes('v=32a') && url.includes('app=smart') && url.includes('region=bac'), url);
  });

  await check('khóa khu: chỉ vùng bac chọn được (và đang active)', async () => {
    const state = await page.evaluate(() => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-region]')).map(b => ({ id: b.dataset.region, disabled: b.disabled, checked: b.getAttribute('aria-checked') })));
    const byId = Object.fromEntries(state.map(s => [s.id, s]));
    assert.equal(byId['bac'].disabled, false);
    assert.equal(byId['bac'].checked, 'true', 'bac phải là khu đang chọn');
    for (const id of ['all', 'trung-tam', 'ven-song', 'nam']) assert.equal(byId[id].disabled, true, id);
  });

  await check('A2: 0 chuỗi cấm trên app=smart', async () => {
    const bad = await scanBanned(page);
    assert.deepEqual(bad, [], 'chuỗi cấm: ' + bad.join(','));
  });

  await check('A2: 0 chuỗi cấm trên web công an + web người dân', async () => {
    for (const path of ['/index-officer.html', '/index-citizen.html']) {
      await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:5173${path}`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
      await sleep(1500);
      const bad = await scanBanned(page);
      assert.deepEqual(bad, [], path + ': ' + bad.join(','));
    }
  });

  await check('A4: vi phạm do perception→trạm sinh → web người dân thấy trong < 10 s (t0 = occurred_at)', async () => {
    // Mở app smart (perception→trạm thật chạy ở tab này) và chờ backend có bản ghi do trạm gửi.
    await page.goto(`${BASE_3D}/index.html?app=smart&v=32a`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
    const officer = await fetch(`${API}/auth/login`, {
      method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ username: 'officer1', password: 'SaiGon@2026' })
    }).then(r => r.json());
    const oh = { Authorization: `Bearer ${officer.access_token}` };
    let created = null;
    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 60 && !created; attempt++) {          // chờ tối đa ~120 s cho trạm sinh vi phạm
      await sleep(2000);
      const list = await fetch(`${API}/officer/violations?page=1&page_size=5`, { headers: oh }).then(r => r.json());
      created = (list.items || [])[0] || null;
    }
    assert(created && created.id, 'trạm chưa sinh vi phạm nào');
    const t0 = Date.parse(created.occurred_at);                          // mốc backend ghi DB (không dùng t_sim/station_time)
    assert(Number.isFinite(t0), 'occurred_at không hợp lệ: ' + created.occurred_at);
    // Công dân chỉ thấy vi phạm `confirmed+` → xác nhận đúng id vừa tạo.
    const conf = await fetch(`${API}/officer/violations/${created.id}/confirm`, {
      method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', ...oh }, body: JSON.stringify({ note: 'A4' })
    }).then(r => r.json());
    assert(conf.status === 'confirmed', JSON.stringify(conf));
    await page.goto('http://127.0.0.1:5173/index-citizen.html', { waitUntil: 'load' });
    await page.fill('input[placeholder*="Biển số"]', created.plate);
    await page.click('button[type=submit]');
    // CHỈ chấp nhận khi thấy ĐÚNG mã vừa tạo (không chấp nhận "Không tìm thấy").
    await page.waitForFunction(id => (document.body.textContent || '').includes(id), created.id, { timeout: 15000 });
    const dt = Date.now() - t0;
    assert(dt < 10000, 'độ trễ e2e ' + dt + ' ms');
  });

  if (process.argv.includes('--fps')) {
    await check('A3: FPS app smart ≥ 90 % FPS region=bac cổ điển (headed, warmup 10 s, trung vị 30 s)', async () => {
      const measure = async url => {
        await page.goto(url, { waitUntil: 'load' });
        await sleep(10000);
        return page.evaluate(() => new Promise(resolve => {
          const samples = [];
          let last = performance.now();
          const tick = now => {
            samples.push(now - last);
            last = now;
            if (samples.length < 30 * 60) requestAnimationFrame(tick);
            else {
              samples.sort((a, b) => a - b);
              resolve(1000 / samples[Math.floor(samples.length / 2)]);
            }
          };
          requestAnimationFrame(tick);
        }));
      };
      // A3: HAI URL cùng góc (overview), cùng khu bac, cùng mức mật độ "Cân bằng" (balanced).
      const classic = await measure(`${BASE_3D}/webgpu-materials-24.html?v=31a&view=overview&region=bac&density=balanced`);
      const smart = await measure(`${BASE_3D}/webgpu-materials-24.html?v=32a&app=smart&view=overview&region=bac&density=balanced`);
      assert(smart / classic >= 0.9, `fps_smart=${smart.toFixed(1)} fps_31a=${classic.toFixed(1)}`);
    });
  }

  await browser.close();
  console.log(JSON.stringify({ checks, failures }, null, 2));
  if (failures.length) process.exit(1);
})();
```

|---|---|---|---|
| A1 | Đèn thích ứng giảm thời gian chờ TB so với cố định | `node scripts/kpi-ab-32.mjs --seed <31031…31035> --scenario peak-hang-xanh --duration 600 --mode fixed|smart --out work/kpi-<mode>-<seed>.json` (headless thuần Node, `regionTrafficPayload('bac')` + `setFraction(1)`); `wait_avg_s` trên `by_node` tập nút được điều khiển cụm Hàng Xanh; `Δwait = (wait_avg_fixed − wait_avg_smart)/wait_avg_fixed` | **X = 15 %**: đạt khi trung bình `Δwait ≥ 15 %` và không seed nào tệ hơn cố định. `8 % ≤ Δwait < 15 %` → X = `Δwait − 2` điểm phần trăm (làm tròn xuống) + ghi `scenario_runs.result_json`; `Δwait < 8 %` → không tự sửa mục tiêu, báo cáo |
| A2 | 0 chuỗi cấm trên UI | `PORT=8821 node outputs/hcmc-poc/scripts/qa-smart-32.cjs` — quét `document.documentElement.textContent` (gồm node ẩn) + `aria-label/title/placeholder/alt` trên `app=smart` và cả 2 web | 0 khớp `giả lập, mô phỏng, minh họa, illustrative, simulated, demo, fake` (không phân biệt hoa/thường) |
| A3 | FPS app smart ≥ 90 % FPS `region=bac` cổ điển | `PORT=8821 node outputs/hcmc-poc/scripts/qa-smart-32.cjs --fps` — **headed trên máy có GPU** (ghi GPU/driver vào báo cáo), warmup 10 s, trung vị 30 s, cùng góc nhìn, mức Cân bằng | `fps_smart / fps_31a ≥ 0,90` |
| A4 | E2E tra cứu thấy vi phạm | Trong `qa-smart-32.cjs`: `t0` = lúc backend ghi DB (`occurred_at`, **không** dùng `t_sim`/`station_time`), đo lúc bản ghi hiện ở web người dân | **< 10 s** |

## Nhật ký sửa sau review

(Sửa theo `work/cx-review-plan.out.md` — 45 phát hiện; mỗi dòng: đã sửa ở đâu, hoặc vì sao không.)

1. N1 T1 — `controller.update` chuyển vào `beginStep()` sau khi tăng `counters.t` (Bước 12 item 7); ghi rõ không gọi trong `step()`.
